# Campaign ledger: every sector as a campaign that asked the parent question

Packet `grut_closed_whole_theory_20261019`. Each sector of the GRUT record was re-read as one campaign
asking: *what does a closed whole, whose subsystem baths are internal complements, force beyond known
physics, in this place?* Each entry below was written by one reader from the primary sources and then
re-read adversarially by a second against the same sources; corrections are recorded in the full entries
under `ledger/`. Classifications: recovered known physics · conditional constraint · supplied model choice ·
rejected mechanism · conjecture · unexecuted task · mixed.

**Answer.** No. On the repository's record the CTP doubling is the standard Feynman–Vernon / Keldysh form that any local causal open system yields (provenance/claims.json:638-655); it does not select the dynamics (GR borrowed, NO_GO_LEDGER.md:59-64; temporal generator supplied, GRUT_WORKING_THEORY_SYNTHESIS_01.md:43), does not fix its own kernel family (S_IF.md:144-151), yields no structure from its own form beyond a presupposed adiabatic mode (calc/RESULTS_L0_redundancy.md:23-33), and does not derive the Born measure (NO_GO_LEDGER.md:66-71; GRUT_PROGRAM_CLOSURE_01.md:38-43; GRUT_III/01_PRINCIPLES.md:66-74). No record uses CTP normalization as a derivation route; the KMS lock and the arrow-existence half are graded standard/imported (GRUT_III/04_LEDGER_MIGRATION.md:14; docs/WHERE_IT_STOPS.md:197). The response vocabulary is 'not physical content' (GRUT_WORKING_THEORY_01.md:84-87) and its one candidate principle is NULL-AS-NEW-PRINCIPLE (:162). The predicted column is empty (GRUT_MODEL_FRAMEWORK.md:86; GRUT_PROGRAM_STATE_SYNTHESIS_01.md:164-165). Later records grade the organization successively as a stance with declared inputs, borrowed-and-verified legs, a Bin-1 bet, a negative-results report, a constitutive framework, a retired ToE claim, a dependency map over nine supplied layers, and in GRUT III an identification inside P0 with S_IF the quadratic approximation of a computed Feynman–Vernon functional. S_IF is downstream of the global law and coupling (P0, P3), a partition/sector choice, a state, access, a supplied quantum lift and ħ; the record's own noise-origin campaign finds the CTP records 'declare no parent' (S2_NOISE_ORIGIN_DEPOSIT_02.md:34). Whether V7 itself asserted anything beyond this cannot be checked: the document is absent from every ref of the repository.

**Closure verdict.** Nothing additional was forced in the stated class. The CTP / S_IF organization is recovered known physics applied to supplied choices, and the repository's own computations show it: (1) form universality, S_IF = K_R + (i/2)N generic, ledger 0 (provenance/claims.json:638-655); (2) the in-in machinery does no work at the gravity rung, EH being one of many Ward-consistent actions (NO_GO_LEDGER.md:59-64; provenance/claims.json:117-133); (3) the doubled-contour action with S1–S7 declared 'fixes nothing' in its modulus space (S_IF.md:144-151), and linearized EH itself is ½k²[P⁽²⁾ − 2P⁽⁰ˢ⁾], ratio −2 (docs/WHERE_IT_STOPS.md:26); (4) the dilatation redundancy of S_IF rests on a presupposed adiabatic mode and L₀ is not the unique breaker (calc/RESULTS_L0_redundancy.md:23-33); (5) the exactly solvable arrow model: Γ(t) even, full recurrence at t_rec ∝ N, decay set by the assumed state (GRUT_ToE.md:160; docs/WHERE_IT_STOPS.md:165-172); (6) decoherence derived exactly but no outcome or weights, EXPERIMENT_P_FRONTIER = CLOSED_AT_TESTED_MODEL_CLASS (GRUT_PROGRAM_CLOSURE_01.md:38-43); (7) hierarchy positivity reduces to state positivity, P-4 21/22, NULL-AS-NEW-PRINCIPLE (GRUT_WORKING_THEORY_01.md:155-165); (8) every differentiator fails by computed magnitude: 7–47, ~22–62 (printed 21–62 elsewhere), 62.7 orders below threshold, 689 Hz withdrawn, 32σ → 2.0σ (GRUT_ToE.md:166; NO_GO_LEDGER.md:54; GRUT_FINAL_SYNTHESIS_01.md:167-170; GRUT_PROGRAM_CLOSURE_01.md:33-37). Boundary: none of these took 'bath = rest of the universe' as premise; they concern a declared split whose bath is 'integrated out and NOT specified' (S_IF.md:21-24) and for which the record declares 'no parent' (S2_HB_OWNER_RULING_02.md:56). This sector has no executed closed-whole computation; the handoff's closed-whole paragraphs are 'a construction brief, not a newly executed general theorem' (handoff:107-109,202-203). 'Nothing additional was forced' is a result, 'not proof that no possible future extra postulate could have content' (handoff:128-129).

### s02_influence_hierarchy — Influence hierarchy / dependence campaigns (kernel, noise, positivity, higher influence data)  (`s02_influence_hierarchy`)

**Question asked.** When a subsystem's bath is the rest of the universe and an observer couples to it only through a declared access structure, what does closure force about the subsystem's influence data (retarded kernel K, noise kernel N, higher connected influence cumulants, and their admissibility) beyond the quantum theory of the closed global system restricted through that coupling? Sub-questions: (a) are kernel and noise downstream of subsystem, state and access choices; (b) does matching two-point data fix higher influence data; (c) are the positivity constraints anything beyond quantum state positivity; (d) what did each verdict establish and in what class. Record framing: 'microscopic dynamics -> influence hierarchy -> sector structure -> access structure? -> effective physics' (P5_ACCESS_CHARTER_01.md:13-14), closing after P-6 as 'access seed = non-derived input' (P6_SEED_SELECTION_VERDICT_01.md:105-110).

**Assumptions / model class.**
- All results are in finite declared classes: D-1 Gaussian class, factorized initial state, |S|=1, finite 9-/11-dim baths (DISCRIMINATOR_VERDICT_01.md:8-9,70-72); P-2 scalar S channel, Gaussian, finite-mode, 'borrowed-standard Gaussian realizability mathematics', 165 sectors = 55 partitions x 3 P-1 worlds (P2_INFLUENCE_CONE_VERDICT_01.md:8-9,41-42; P2_INFLUENCE_CONE_RESULT.json:133); P-3/P-4/P-5 exact finite spin baths with dephasing probe in the 'declared finite bounded-access class' (P4_HIERARCHY_CHARTER_01.md:11-13; P5_ACCESS_CHARTER_01.md:6-7,25-71); P-6 exact finite spin systems, noncommuting seed algebras, entropy/complexity/least-action/simplicity selectors prohibited (P6_SEED_SELECTION_CHARTER_01.md:17-26,38-110).
- C1-a/C1-a2: finite first-order passive local networks, stepped modulation, declared window (C1_STATIONARITY_SEAM_VERDICT_01.md:88-93; C1A2_PACKAGING_VERDICT_01.md:90-92). Kernel transport / nonstationarity: free TT graviton commutator kernel per comoving k on declared LCDM Om=0.315, OL=0.685, fixed-comoving-k object, no smearing (KERNEL_TRANSPORT_RESULT.json:8-11,234; KERNEL_NONSTATIONARITY_RESULT.json:282). EQ-1: 1+1 only, declared five-direction family (EQ1_EQUIVALENCE_VERDICT_01.md:118-123). S4-1: finite chains L=8, range<=3 (S41_SEL4_VERDICT_01.md:89-97). FS-1: exact phonon ring N=20, quadratic translation-invariant charges to range 4, 'Everything below is conditional on C_cons' (FS1_FREE_VERDICT_01.md:10-11,31-34,85-87). CA-1: 1D ring substrates, IR static and short-time data (CA1_CARRIER_VERDICT_01.md:80-81). S2 chain: declared nonlinear C-B class with supplied L0-1c drift (S2_OWNER_RULING_03.md:47-49).
- Supplied inputs presupposed throughout: physical hbar ('located, not generated', P2_S1_OWNER_RULING_01.md:37-43); the access seed as a declaration / irreducible input (P5_ACCESS_VERDICT_01.md:35-38; G1_GEOMETRY_CHARTER_01.md:4-5); the quantum class/lift (S5_OWNER_RULING_01.md:27-35); on FRW a state choice, a KMS/temperature structure and a stationary reduction (KERNEL_TRANSPORT_VERDICT_01.md:41-51; GRUT_FORMALIZATION_02.md:109); on the gravity side Sel-4, C_cons as supplied probe field content, GeoInv (EQ1_EQUIVALENCE_VERDICT_01.md:13-22; CC1_CCONS_VERDICT_01.md:15-17; U1_UNIVERSALITY_CHARTER_01.md:3-9).
- The influence functional is computed, not postulated: 'the reduced probe dynamics is a functional of the contour-ordered influence functional BY CONSTRUCTION (Feynman-Vernon at operator level)' (P4_HIERARCHY_CHARTER_01.md:74-77); GRUT III P1 'supplies no influence functional and no dynamics' and 'No kernel is supplied anywhere; nothing is earned by that fact alone, because the kernels that replace it are standard physics' (GRUT_III/01_PRINCIPLES.md:29-31,86-88).

**Executed evidence.**
- D-1 (13/13): non-isomorphic worlds (9-dim vs 11-dim) with identical (K,N) are S-indistinguishable; kernel-equal state-different worlds are distinguishable only through N; 'The equivalence-class label is the pair (K, N) — in non-Gaussian worlds, the full influence functional — never K alone.' — `DISCRIMINATOR_VERDICT_01.md:6,20-47,60-67; DISCRIMINATOR_D1_RESULT.json:12,22,37,57,62,84-104; D1_P2_OWNER_RULING_01.md:9-10,18-19` — Delta_K = 6.3e-15, Delta_N < 1e-8, Delta_1 = 4.5e-15, Delta_2 = 6.3e-15, W-D: Delta_K = 0.0 (same K, thermal state; 'state-independence of the kernel'), noise kernel differs by 1.09, Delta_1(A,D) = 0.91

**Answer.** (a) Yes for the influence functional, with two precisions. The influence data are the Feynman-Vernon output of a supplied (global law, state, coupling) (P4_HIERARCHY_CHARTER_01.md:74-77; GRUT_III/01_PRINCIPLES.md:29-31,41-43). The subsystem criterion moves a sector to a different point of the same cone (P-2 L-D, 165 sectors); the access seed is a declaration that no tested dynamical selector fixes beyond block decomposition (P-5; P-6 NON-DERIVED INPUT, 0.287 at identical closures, PATH-DEPENDENT minimal seeds) and in-access data cannot fix out-of-access physics (P-5, 1.547); the formalization records K_R = K_R(M, a) with partition dependence explicit (GRUT_FORMALIZATION_02.md:63). But in the Gaussian/free classes where it was tested, K is state-independent (D-1 Delta_K = 0.0 ground vs thermal; FRW SL(2,R) invariance 1.1e-13); the state enters through N (1.09) and, beyond Gaussianity, the record says only that the label becomes 'the full influence functional — never K alone' (DISCRIMINATOR_VERDICT_01.md:39-40); K's own state dependence outside those classes is not separately tested. Nothing in closure selects the law, state, coupling or seed. (b) No: matched (K,N) with different kappa_4 separate by 0.338; matching through order 2n leaves order-(2n+2) discrimination (17.2, 54.9; order-8 frozen gate red at 67.8 with post-hoc 325->320->276); the same ladder recurs at seed level (P-6, 54.9) and in EQ-1 (4.035, 16.65). Full-hierarchy matching certifies only in-class by cited bounded-moment determinacy, and the 'no untracked pair constructible' claim was never executed (P4_HIERARCHY_CORRECTION_01.md:45-49,83-85). In the converse direction, FS-1 shows that for conserved couplings in a free sector dynamic influence data of every order are identically blind (J(omega!=0) = 0) and only static multi-temperature or geometric access distinguishes (FS1_FREE_VERDICT_01.md:42-62). (c) No: the scalar floor (state-positivity check), the matrix cone (Gram reconstruction 5.6e-17), the full Gram hierarchy (vacuum on the face at 0.0e+00), the access algebra (bicommutant; central decomposition in P-6) and the off-stationary admissibility (Gram PSD 8.6e-14) all reduce to positivity of the global state restricted through the declared coupling; CP is dilation-guaranteed; R2 is graded '[IDENTITY] + [DERIVED-IN-CLASS]' (GRUT_FORMALIZATION_02.md:64); hbar is the floor height and is supplied; owner-binding: 'The cone is geometry of physically realizable influence data, not an independent explanation of quantum mechanics.' The one place positivity forces something on the gravity side — conservation of the probe coupling — it does so only given a supplied massless gauge-field probe (CC1_CCONS_VERDICT_01.md:15-17). (d) Per-verdict classes are tabulated in the ledger file §6: every positive result is DERIVED-IN-CLASS or [IDENTITY] in a finite declared class; every selector attack on the hierarchy (EQ-1, S4-1, FS-1, CC-1 constant level, P-6) returned NULL or reduced to a supplied input; the kernel on FRW is a recomputation from admitted inputs with state/KMS/stationary reduction supplied.

**Closure verdict.** Nothing additional was forced in this sector's stated classes. The computation that shows it: (1) every admissibility constraint found on influence data (J>=0, nu>=hbar J/2, nu+-J/2>=0, complete Gram positivity) was exhibited as the restriction of a positive global state through the declared coupling (P-2 verdict :22-27; P-3 Gram reconstruction 5.6e-17; P-4 vacuum 0.0e+00 / thermal 0.6878; C1-a Gram 8.6e-14 off the stationary domain). The redundancy rule was made binding from P-3 onward (P3_NC_LIFT_VERDICT_01.md:27-30; P4_HIERARCHY_CHARTER_01.md:8-10); P-2 itself is CONE-CONFIRMED / DERIVED-IN-CLASS given hbar, and its reduction to state positivity is demonstrated by P-4's two-point face, so that P-5 reads the 'NULL-AS-NEW-PRINCIPLE pattern of P-2/P-3/P-4' (P5_ACCESS_VERDICT_01.md:57-59) — known quantum mechanics in influence coordinates. (2) Closure with a declared subsystem leaves the complement free: at fixed (K,N) non-isomorphic worlds are S-indistinguishable to 4.5e-15 (D-1); at an identical in-access full hierarchy the out-of-access physics differs by 1.547 and appears in-access (0.38) only when the access boundary moves (P-5); identical dynamical closures hide physically different seeds (0.287) and minimal sufficient seeds are path-dependent (P-6). Owner-binding wording: 'the access boundary is currently an unresolved structural input'; 'access is arbitrary bookkeeping' FALSIFIED in-class; 'access is dynamically selected' NOT established; after P-6 'access seed = irreducible input, not a new law' (P6_SEED_SELECTION_CHARTER_01.md:3-9; G1_GEOMETRY_CHARTER_01.md:4-5). The P-5 instrument's phrase 'a physical boundary condition' is not owner wording. (3) The kernel is a recomputation from the supplied global law and background (FRW: A4+A7); what is missing on an evolving background is supplied structure (state, KMS, stationary reduction), not a new law; stationarity is a state property. (4) A nonstationary microscopic law yields a two-time kernel requiring a two-time datum (C1-a/C1-a2, in-class); the single spectral measure is a special case, not forced; the datum's minimality is unproved. (5) The hierarchy does not select the gravity-side coupling: c_full NULL in EQ-1, S4-1, FS-1 and at CC-1's constant level; what selects is supplied (Sel-4; C_cons reduced to a massless gauge-field probe; GeoInv). Surviving descriptive dividends, not promoted: one Gram condition replaces the cone's two axioms as low-order faces; the cumulant ladder's lambda-scaling on constructed pairs; the in-class elimination of the classical relaxational carrier. The physically load-bearing finding is the access seed under boundary change (P-5) and its non-selectability by any tested principle beyond block decomposition (P-6), with entropy/complexity/least-action/simplicity selectors not evaluated and the result 'NOT generalized to QFT/type-III': the subsystem's own influence data do not fix 'the rest', and no principle in the record fixes it.

**Classification.** `mixed` — Dominantly recovered_known_physics: positivity constraints are state positivity (P-2/P-3/P-4/P-5/P-6/C1-a); the influence functional is computed by Feynman-Vernon, not postulated; the FRW kernel is a free-field recomputation; P-2 is borrowed-standard Gaussian realizability mathematics. Conditional constraints in finite declared classes: the kappa_4 class split and cumulant ladder on constructed pairs; the Gaussian cone given hbar; the two-time datum at finite stepped level; R = 0.516 on the frozen grid; finite-order pair data cannot decide Sel-4 compliance; FS-1's identically blind dynamic data for conserved couplings (given C_cons); CC-1's positivity => conservation only for a massless gauge-field probe. Supplied model choices: hbar; the access seed and coupling operators (P-5/P-6); which hierarchy point per sector (amplitude, state, boundary data); FRW state, KMS/temperature and stationary reduction; Sel-4; C_cons as probe field content (CC-1, governing over the earlier 'unresolved import'); GeoInv. Rejected mechanisms: classical relaxational carrier (within the supplied quantum class, CA-1; escape open); every local dS->FRW substitution rule (34-130%); Dt-only packaging at finite stepped level (C1-a2); minimality/dynamical closure as a seed selector (P-6); initial uncertainty as a substitute for ongoing nonlinear noise (S2-1). Unexecuted tasks: the P-4 descriptiveness search (never run); P-6 prohibited selectors; C1-b and C1-c; minimality of the mixing datum; the smeared worldline nonstationarity object; a physically constrained bath for the C-B process (new-parent rescue, not authorized); S4-1 small-g robustness; FS-1 non-quadratic charges; the type-III generalization (map, not claims). No conjecture is promoted; 'the fundamental object is the cumulant hierarchy' stays a CANDIDATE.

**Remaining freedom.**
- Which hierarchy point reality realizes per sector: amplitude, state, boundary data (P4_HIERARCHY_VERDICT_01.md:72-76; SYN1_OWNER_RULING_01.md:63-66).
- The value of hbar, the floor height — located, not generated (P2_S1_OWNER_RULING_01.md:37-43).
- The access seed — selected by dynamics only up to block decomposition; beyond that 'interface-relative, order-relative, path-dependent, degeneracy-ridden' — and all out-of-access structure at fixed in-access hierarchy (P6_SEED_SELECTION_VERDICT_01.md:105-117; P5_ACCESS_VERDICT_01.md:77-85); entropy/complexity/least-action/simplicity selectors not evaluated (P6 :110-112).
- The full influence hierarchy beyond any finite matched order, and whether a matched-full-hierarchy pair with untracked physics exists — unsearched (P4_HIERARCHY_VERDICT_01.md:43-48; P4_HIERARCHY_CORRECTION_01.md:83-85).
- On FRW: the state choice (no Bunch-Davies analog), the KMS/temperature structure (no timelike Killing field), the proved stationary reduction; the smeared worldline object (KERNEL_TRANSPORT_VERDICT_01.md:41-51; NONSTATIONARITY_RESULT_01.md:51-55).
- Minimality of the two-time mixing datum; C1-b and C1-c (C1A2_PACKAGING_VERDICT_01.md:93-95; GRUT_FORMALIZATION_02.md:336,339-340).
- The physical origin of the noise: no HB-P parent on the record (S2_HB_OWNER_RULING_02.md:93-102).
- The type-III boundary: operator-valued spectral measures, no factorized preparations without the split-property collar — 'map, not claims'; P-6's seed result 'NOT generalized to QFT/type-III' (P3_NC_LIFT_VERDICT_01.md:79-88; G1_GEOMETRY_CHARTER_01.md:7-8).

### s03_level0_floor — Level-0 floor and earned core theorems (access rank-drop, DORD, DHERM, termination)  (`s03_level0_floor`)

**Question asked.** Do the Level-0 formulability-floor theorems (O-1 … O-7), the access rank-drop theorems, the D-ORD theorems and the floor termination condition constrain a closed whole, whose every subsystem's bath is the rest of the universe, beyond standard quantum theory — or are they theorems about supplied classes (access structure, generator/kernel families)? The floor's own recorded question is 'Can the system even be formulated without already assuming ordering, a generator, and determinism?' (L0_1_FLOOR_DESIGN_01.md:26-27), scoped to 'the linear relaxational class ẋ = −Kx on a finite substrate … Unitary quantum generators (H Hermitian) are a different object; … deferred, not designed here' (L0_1_FLOOR_DESIGN_01.md:15-20). Per-obligation questions: O-1 (L0_1D_CHARTER_01.md:21-23), O-5 (L0_1F_DORD_THEOREM_01.md:22-24), O-6 (L0_1G_CHARTER_01.md:20-24), O-7 (L0_1_FLOOR_O7_ADJUDICATION_01.md:78-80), rank-drop (L0_ACCESS_RANKDROP_THEOREM_01.md:18-19).

**Assumptions / model class.**
- Checkout: working tree a6be0e4, five WIP commits past the handoff commit 96a7e7e; git diff --stat 96a7e7e HEAD over every cited sector file is empty, so line numbers hold at 96a7e7e.
- Common substrate: K_b, the sealed C1 bath, (1:,1:) block of build_K(24,0), 23×23 symmetric irreducible tridiagonal positive definite, K = 0.3I + path Laplacian, λ_k = 2.3 − 2cos((2k−1)π/47), λ₁ ≈ 0.30447 (L0_1E_THEOREM_01.md:18-20, 43-48); retained observable is site 1, an end of the path (L0_ACCESS_RANKDROP_THEOREM_01.md:47-49).
- O-1 class: ring K_s = K_b + (e₁ − e₂₃)(e₁ − e₂₃)ᵀ; deformation K_{i,i+1} = −(1 − γa_i), K_{i+1,i} = −(1 + γa_i); C(γ) with 𝒜 = 23·ln((1+γ)/(1−γ)), γ_C ∈ {0, 0.1, 0.3, 0.6, 0.9}; B(γ) with 𝒜 = 0, γ_B ∈ {0.3, 0.9}; T(γ), γ_T ∈ {0, 0.9}; K_s held so accretivity holds by construction; every member Metzler; pre-stated theorem: affinity is necessary for a CM breach (L0_1D_CHARTER_01.md:32-36, 56-58, 69-88).
- O-2 class: attached one-way ring K(d,g) = d·I + a·E₁₁ − g·Q, not derived from K_b; n = 23, a = 1, g ∈ {1/20, 1/10, 1/4, 1/2, 1, 2, 4, 10}, exact fractions (L0_1H_DHERM_B_DESIGN_01.md:30-38; L0_1H_THEOREM_01.md:90-92). Theorem hypotheses: J-4 needs n odd and n ≥ 4 (DESIGN:65-75); P-1 needs −K Metzler irreducible and a stable member d > w* (DESIGN:82-89); P-2 needs n ≥ 3, a > 0 (DESIGN:98-106); J-6 needs P = gQ + a(I − E₁₁) ≥ 0 irreducible aperiodic (THEOREM:48-71). H-HERM-2 split into H2-m/H2-s/H2-n before evaluation (DESIGN:23-26, 139-143); mapping confirmed before the freeze (L0_1H_OWNER_RULING_02.md:8-9).
- O-3/O-4 class: OU dx = −Kx dt + B dW, Q = 2·diag(T_i), T_i declared rationals 'never constructed from K and never set from rung2's KMS lock' (L0_1E_THEOREM_01.md:21-23); T-0 needs only linear drift, additive noise, −K Hurwitz (:34-41); scope clause on every O-4 line (:153-156).
- O-5 classes: 𝒞₁ ẋ = −Kx with −K Hurwitz (pin = 0 and negative-spring members excluded by name); 𝒞₂ gradient convex quartic; 𝒞₃ stationary linear-Gaussian with all variables even under time reversal; conservative classes out of scope (L0_1F_DORD_THEOREM_01.md:42-62). D-3 needs f locally Lipschitz, V ∈ C¹, V̇ < 0 off equilibria (:115-117); D-4 needs a continuous flow and α(γ)∩ω(γ) ≠ ∅ (:166-170); D-5 needs OU stationarity and even variables (:217-218, 244-246). 'The generator is therefore a presupposition on this document's face, not something it derives' (:34-35); no computation on any record member (:8).
- O-6 class: conservative pinned harmonic chain q̈ = −K_N q, diagonal 2.3/1.3, off-diagonal −1, K₂₃ = K_b, N ∈ {23, 47, 95}, system = site 1, g = 1, zero-mean Gaussian product of uncoupled Gibbs states, pairs (2,1),(10,1),(1/2,1),(1/10,1); 'No anharmonic or quantum substrates' (L0_1G_CHARTER_01.md:45-61, 152-153). The charter was never frozen (:3-5).
- Access rank-drop: f_k = −d_k x_k − g_k(x_k) + w_{k−1}x_{k−1} + w_k x_{k+1}, every w_k ≠ 0, any smooth on-site g_k, readout h = x₁; part (iv) needs forward completeness (L0_ACCESS_RANKDROP_THEOREM_01.md:57-69); Theorem 2 adds a priced probe g = e₁ (:97-102).

**Answer.** Every theorem in this sector is a theorem about a supplied class, and none is formulated on a quantum state space. (1) The floor's E1–E4 edges, O-1 (affinity vs asymmetry on the K_b-derived 23-site ring), O-2 (accretivity vs stability on the separate one-way ring K(d,g), n = 23), O-3/O-4 (OU with declared diagonal noise on K_b) and O-5 (static presentations, Lyapunov order, lag distinguishability for 𝒞₁/𝒞₂/𝒞₃) all presuppose a declared first-order dissipative generator, a declared end-site readout and a declared noise rule; the design places unitary quantum generators outside the floor (L0_1_FLOOR_DESIGN_01.md:15-20); the generator is 'a presupposition on this document's face' (L0_1F_DORD_THEOREM_01.md:34-35), later ruled IRREDUCIBLE/SUPPLIED (S5_OWNER_RULING_03.md:99-100). (2) Rank-drop Theorem 1 is identity-grade at the declared readout (det DΦ = ∏ w_k^{N−k} = 1 for K_b), ruled TRIVIAL/IDENTITY and not to be generalized; Theorem 2 is probe-dependent, 'not ontology' (L0_ACCESS_BRIDGE_OWNER_RULING_02.md:13-14, 57-71). (3) The one obligation that tests a closed whole with an internal bath, O-6 (classical conservative harmonic chain + partition + product preparation), returns a non-forcing result: strict pointwise ordering is FALSIFIED by band-edge memory (ruled from identities plus a non-member asymptotic check; the charter was never frozen or run), the past hypothesis gives an anti-arrow at t = 0⁺, and the later S6-1 net arrow (N = ∞ primary, N = 95 non-adjudicating cross-check) is integrated, ensemble and preparation-relative (L0_1G_OWNER_RULING_02.md:9-41; S6_OWNER_RULING_02.md:100-102, 120-121; S6_1_VERDICT_01.md:95-98). (4) O-7 shows the floor's own two-property reduction fails and what survives is a list of non-implications inside declared classes (L0_1_FLOOR_O7_OWNER_RULING_01.md:10-35). (5) The termination condition 'executes nothing' (L0_1_FLOOR_TERMINATION_DRAFT_01.md:82-85); it is a stopping rule, not a physical statement. Therefore no theorem in this sector constrains a closed whole beyond standard quantum theory; what the sector recovered is standard classical dynamical-systems, OU and harmonic-chain mathematics, certified exactly on a small set of declared families (K_b, its ring, the one-way ring, the pinned chain), plus the explicit record that the generator, readout, noise origin, preparation and physical lift remain supplied.

**Closure verdict.** Nothing additional was forced in the stated classes. The computation that shows it is the floor's own record: (a) D-4 — on any orbit with α(γ)∩ω(γ) ≠ ∅ no continuous state function is strictly monotone; every orbit is recurrent for positive-definite quadratic Hamiltonians and finite-dimensional unitary dynamics, 'Not every conservative system is recurrent (a free particle is not), so the statement is scoped' (L0_1F_DORD_THEOREM_01.md:166-175, 197-202); (b) O-6 — in the conservative pinned chain with partition and uncorrelated product start, strict ordering of heat current and relative entropy fails (J bounded by t⁻³; D, Ḋ leading t⁻⁶; non-member check J < 0 on 49.6% of the late window, 739 sign changes) and the past hypothesis ≠ arrow at t = 0 (L0_1G_OWNER_RULING_02.md:9-38; L0_1G_CORRECTIONS_01.md:18-24; L0_1G_PREFREEZE_REVIEW_01.md:35-39); what survives is an integrated, preparation-relative net direction on the infinite chain (S6_OWNER_RULING_02.md:100-102, 120-121); (c) P-1 — retained-site response on the one-way ring cannot see state-norm growth, an observability identity (L0_1H_DHERM_B_DESIGN_01.md:82-95); (d) the generator is presupposed and later ruled IRREDUCIBLE/SUPPLIED (L0_1F_DORD_THEOREM_01.md:29-35; S5_OWNER_RULING_03.md:99-100); (e) the readout is declared and observability at it is an identity (L0_ACCESS_BRIDGE_OWNER_RULING_02.md:13, 59-60); (f) no earned structure selects the physical lift (L0_ACCESS_BRIDGE_OWNER_RULING_02.md:30; L0_POSTFLOOR_DEPOSIT_01.md:23). Of the floor's own hypotheses, H-HERM-2, strict H-ORD and the two-property reduction failed; the generator, access structure and lift were post-floor gates that returned SUPPLIED. The sector recovered known mathematics and classical physics only; the floor 'makes no claim about the axioms of reality' (L0_1_FLOOR_DEPOSIT_01.md:142-143), and 'nothing forced' is 'not proof that no possible future extra postulate could have content' (NEXT_CHAT_HANDOFF.md:128-129).

**Classification.** `mixed` — Recovered known physics/mathematics: D-1/D-1′ (Laplace, Kronecker, Ho–Kalman, Lanczos), D-3 (strict Lyapunov ordering), D-4 (α∩ω recurrence obstruction; Conley cited), D-5 (OU reversibility ⟺ KΣ = ΣKᵀ), T-0/T-4 (Gaussian regression theorem, FDT member Σ = K⁻¹), T-2 (Bernstein), T-6 (cone from linearity in T), T-11 (OU detailed-balance break), P-1 (Perron bound on Metzler generators), J-4, O-6's band-edge memory and I-5 slip, S-6 return to equilibrium (Riemann–Lebesgue on an a.c. spectrum). Conditional constraint (declared class only): E1–E4 edges; O-1 CLASS-SPLIT; O-2 nesting w* < d_acc < d_mono at 8 g; O-4 cone location (R* ≈ 83.16, ≈ 9800, ∞; 22 steps pass); Theorem 1 at the earned readout; S-6 net arrow (preparation-relative, N = ∞). Supplied model choice: generator and orientation (A-3/A-4), readout/access seed (A-13), the declared noise rule and its physical origin (A-11), the ring/one-way-ring families, O-6's preparation and partition (A-14), the lift (A-5). Rejected mechanism: H-HERM-2 (O-2), strict H-ORD (O-6), the two-property reduction (O-7), H-PROJ as cross-program explanation (forest layer). Unexecuted task: the O-6 chartered strict-gate run on N ∈ {23, 47, 95} and the K-3 drawdown map (ARBITRARY); K_b interior-site rank-drop sets; S-4, S-7, S-8 not selected; faithful-representation lift audit preserved, not opened. Conjecture (fenced by the owner): linearity as an 'effective mathematical regime' ('suggests'); the ring-lap mechanism for d_mono.

**Remaining freedom.**
- The temporal generator kind and its sign/orientation remain supplied (S5_OWNER_RULING_03.md:99-100; L0_1F_DORD_THEOREM_01.md:256-260; S5_OWNER_RULING_01.md:54-57).
- The access seed / readout interface; multi-site access sets are 'declared … that the L0 records do not earn' (L0_ACCESS_BRIDGE_CORRECTIONS_01.md:10-12; LEVEL0_FOREST_SYNTHESIS_01.md:49-54, 144).

### s04_grut_I_II — GRUT I–II vacuum response (recovery with imports, projector choice, conditional exclusions, supplied arrow) — VERIFIED AND CORRECTED  (`s04_grut_I_II`)

**Question asked.** Closed-whole form as assigned: in the responsive-vacuum sector, what did treating the gravitational vacuum as an open system whose bath is the rest of the universe force beyond known physics — what was recovered only with imports, what was a projector choice, which exclusions are conditional, and is the arrow supplied? Retained: the campaign asked different questions — V1 'how far is the responsiveness bet forced to reach?' and V2 'What is universal about constitutive structure itself, before any microscopic ontology?' (GRUT_II_Agenda.md:3-7); no primary source poses 'bath = rest of the universe'; the bath is a declared KMS equilibrium bath ('borrowed standard + declared equilibrium', GRUT_PREDICTION_GATE_GAMMA_T.md:92) with open pole structure ('single pole vs branch cut ... The bath.', GRUT_I_What_Survived.md:55). The closed-whole reading is imposed by the 2026-10-19 handoff, itself 'a construction brief, not a newly executed general theorem' (NEXT_CHAT_HANDOFF.md:8-16, 107-109).

**Assumptions / model class.**
- Ontological bet, not derivation: the vacuum 'is a responsive open quantum system — a Schwinger–Keldysh influence action with a system↔bath split (rung1) ... a Bin-1 primitive ... not a derivation' (GRUT_I_What_Survived.md:13, 53). Live register (Ruling B split 2026-08-23): rung1_inin_formalism tier derived-pending, ledger_delta 4; rung1_ontology_finite_memory 'a STANCE, explicitly not derived', tier assumed, +1 (provenance/claims.json:5, 11, 28, 34).
- Equilibrium declared: FDT/KMS detailed balance 'as an admission gate' (rung2) (GRUT_I_What_Survived.md:14, 54); 'KMS lock + T_dS = H/2π — borrowed standard + declared equilibrium' (GRUT_PREDICTION_GATE_GAMMA_T.md:92); this gate 'is exactly the object where the direction enters (β>0)' (ARROW_OF_TIME.md:70). Live tier derived-pending, ledger_delta −1 ('N removed as an independent input ... T is NOT booked as an independent input anywhere, DELIBERATELY') (provenance/claims.json:47 node).
- Finite memory 'a posit within the rung1 ontology'; pole vs cut open, rung3 derived-pending (GRUT_I_What_Survived.md:15, 55).
- Pure-TT projector 'CHOSEN, not forced'; family K = c₂P^TT + c₀P⁰ˢ with c₀ = 0 'an input, not a consequence'; 'GRUT keeps one survivor and zeroes the other BY HAND (on the FRW frame mu_linear uses: 5 independent form factors, GRUT keeps 1, zeroes 4)' (GRUT_I_What_Survived.md:65; GRUT_II_What_Survived.md:43; provenance/claims.json:319).
- Known limits recovered by import: GR via Jacobson/Clausius 'borrowed, not hosted (Ward identity can't fix the action)' (rung5, tier assumed, ledger_delta 2); QM via master equation, 'the Born measure is an inherited primitive' (rung6, tier assumed, ledger_delta 2) (GRUT_I_What_Survived.md:57-58; provenance/claims.json:117, 142 nodes).
- Arrow direction imported as the Past Hypothesis, 'a Bin-1 primitive'; arrow_of_time tier assumed, ledger_delta 1, 'NOT derived; LOCATED' (GRUT_I_What_Survived.md:63; provenance/claims.json:262 node, :281).
- Γ_T-sector inputs: τ₂ ~ 1/H₀ 'INSERTED, un-sourced (+2 of rung7's +3)'; B 'STAKED illustrative; two live values 3.2 orders apart (0.4 vs ~2.4e-4)'; B ≡ ε 'UNVERIFIED'; ω_c 'UNPINNED across 39.6 orders'; 'single FRW clock | declared (C5 obligation)' (GRUT_PREDICTION_GATE_GAMMA_T.md:93-97).
- Noise-kernel transversality: since 2026-08-14 a named input ('NOT bought by the diagonal Ward identity; it is an input'), derived only on the booked family, conditional on a +1 priced 2026-08-17 (provenance/claims.json:319).

### s05_grut_III — GRUT III / observer bath (P0–P5, P1 identification, modular KMS, GR recovery, universal coupling)  (`s05_grut_III`)

**Question asked.** Once "every subsystem's bath is the rest of the universe" is realised algebraically as P1 (the observer's medium is (A(O), ω|_{A(O)}, σ^ω_t); the bath is the commutant A(O)′, = A(O′) under Haag duality), do P0–P5 force any state, kernel, coupling, constant, arrow or outcome rule that GR + QFT in curved spacetime does not already contain? Sub-questions: is P1 an identification or a law; is modular KMS a temperature for a physical clock; what is imported in GR recovery and universal coupling; what do the GRUT III predictions depend on. (GRUT_III/README.md:13-16; NEXT_CHAT_HANDOFF.md:92-94)

**Assumptions / model class.**
- P0: local relativistic QFT on a Lorentzian manifold, net of von Neumann algebras, Einstein causality, covariance, spectrum condition, Hadamard states; bundles Lorentzian signature, Hilbert structure and field content (GRUT_III/01_PRINCIPLES.md:14-21).
- P1 (identification, 0 credit): medium = (A(O), ω|_{A(O)}, modular flow); bath = commutant; local algebras type III₁, so no tensor factorisation, no reduced density matrix, no influence functional, no dynamics (GRUT_III/01_PRINCIPLES.md:23-31).
- P2: S = ηA and Clausius across every local causal horizon; area law IMPORTED; η = 1/(4Għ) inserted (datum G) (GRUT_III/01_PRINCIPLES.md:45-52).
- P3: vertex (κ/2)h_μν T^μν with κ² = 32πG inserted; universality = operational equivalence principle (GRUT_III/01_PRINCIPLES.md:54-59).
- P4: future horizon traced out, β > 0 (Past Hypothesis, located not derived); P5: non-contextuality → Gleason/Busch Born rule (GRUT_III/01_PRINCIPLES.md:61-74).
- Data: ħ, c, G, k_B, Λ, particle spectrum; GRUT III does not derive G (GRUT_III/01_PRINCIPLES.md:10-12).
- Every computed result: free conformal or massless minimally coupled fields, Bunch–Davies/conformal vacuum, geodesic/comoving worldlines, exact dS or power-law FRW with constant ε, two-point (Gaussian) level, fixed-mean-time (Wigner) spectra at ω ≠ 0 (GRUT_III/02_DERIVATIONS.md:259-266; GRUT_III/07_ACCESS_RESOLVED_RESPONSE.md:126-128).

**Answer.** (1) P1 is an identification, not a law: zero postulate credit, no dynamics, no influence functional, no type-III factorisation, and no state-selection condition; the restriction map exists for every global state, so P1 cannot fix even a local one-point function (GRUT_III/01_PRINCIPLES.md:23-43; CLOSURE_SELECTION_RESPONSE.md:7-11,28-40). (2) Modular KMS is automatic for any faithful state and is a temperature for a physical clock only where the modular flow is geometric — each such case an imported theorem (Bisognano–Wichmann, Kay–Wald, Gibbons–Hawking, Hislop–Longo) (01:30-39; CLOSURE_SELECTION_RESPONSE.md:42-45). In FRW with ε ≠ 0 the apparent horizon is not Killing and P1 assigns no temperature; what is computable is conventional QFT: the comoving detector's Wigner spectrum is KMS at (ä/ȧ)/2π to O(ε) in both conformal (v11) and massless (v14) sectors, a one-sided instantaneous rate differs at O(ε), T_KH is a separate near-horizon scaling-limit KMS temperature, and the exact ΛCDM kernel today is KMS at no β (RESULTS.md:610-620,644-663,787-798; 02:194-196,213-221; 13:163-167). (3) GR recovery imports the area law, the Jacobson 1995 derivation and Hadamard/Kay–Wald thermality and inserts η = 1/(4Għ) via the Clausius postulate P2; Λ is an integration constant; one-loop induced gravity with SM content gives Σk₁ = +1/6 or −1/2, so G is not derived. Universal coupling inserts κ² = 32πG (P3); its derived content Im Π(s) = c_eff(s)Gs² with weights 1:3:12 is contained in Han–Lykken–Zhang 1999 (01:45-59; 02:60-69,199-209; 08:119-138). (4) The GRUT III predictions depend on P0's field content, the Bunch–Davies/Hadamard state, the imported horizon-thermality theorems, the inserted G and κ, and (D11/D13) the slow-roll hypothesis in the Wigner convention; F1–F6 are all shared with ΛCDM / GR + QFT and 'GRUT III makes no prediction that distinguishes it from GR + QFT in curved spacetime' (03:9,13-24). **Closure verdict.** Nothing additional was forced in the stated class (covariant QFT with Hadamard states, bath = commutant / causal complement, KMS states), and the sector contains the computations that show it. Realising 'bath = rest of the universe' as P1 forces only tautological KMS in the modular parameter; by the FDT identity N_f = ½coth(βω/2)ρ_f any extra noise at the same β is extra dissipation, i.e. a change of field content (03:5-9; 07:20-38). In every regime computed — exact dS (v8: KMS defect 2.41e-35, residual template EMPTY), quasi-dS conformal sector through O(ε²) (v11: D/ε → 2πω/H̄ to ≲1e-9), quasi-dS massless sector at O(ε) (v14: residual halving ratios → 4 only at H_eff), horizon first laws (v12: identities of the Einstein equations) — 'the answer is fixed by conventional theory and P0–P5 add no selection' (05:123-125). No state is selected (restriction exists for every state; the parameter-free SJ candidate violates P2 and the CMB; the DMP horizon state is unproven at ν = 3/2), no constant is fixed (G inserted, Λ an integration constant, Sakharov fails with SM content), and P4 (arrow) and P5 (Born) remain postulates. Closure cannot make the complement thermal by fiat: 'before there is a geometry there is no bath — the universe has no outside' (13:60-62). What survives is GR + QFT with a thermodynamic reading, the Δρ residual instrument, and a priced ten-line constraint sheet for any sixth postulate; this is not a theorem that no future postulate could have content — the state door (N1) and the renormalisation-constant door (N2) are pre-registered, unexecuted (12:36-105; 13:357-375).

**Classification.** `mixed` — Dominated by recovered known physics (D1–D4, D7, D8, D10, D12 IMPORTED; T1–T6 standard; 12:15-25). Conditional constraints: D11 first-order thermality (ε, η first-order small, Wigner convention, ω ≫ εH, not today), D13 (constant ε, comoving worldline, ω ≠ 0, massless scalar; general slow roll and gravitons not established), D5 (√s variable, illustrative staircase), F1–F6 as falsifiers of the minimal framework only. Supplied model choices: P0 content and data, η = 1/(4Għ), κ² = 32πG, P4, P5, the Bunch–Davies/Hadamard state, the fixed-mean-time convention. Rejected mechanisms: a₀ via Jacobson, memory time 1/(4H), bulk Planckian fluid, c_eff at LIGO frequencies, Born via HJW, w ≡ −1 as discriminator, '+16 → +5 compression'/'GR hosted'/'frontier dissolved', v11 rev. 2 coefficient 1.01, v13 rev. 1 inference, the record's rung-3 and S5-1 kernels (SUPPLIED), the sharp Sorkin–Johnston state. Conjecture: P1 selecting the DMP horizon state; a non-inserted residual in the interacting gravitational infrared; N1, N2. Unexecuted tasks: O7 items 1, 2, 4; literature check for r(ω) and −(1 + 2 ln 2); first-principles derivation of the v13 13.5 invariant; general slow-roll and tensor extensions of D13; O5 item 2; SJ region ending today; N1–N4; independent review of CP-04/CP-05; closure fork B Step 1.

### s06_cp12_geometry — CP-12 geometry / preparation (geometry-selection obstructions, min-entropy extension)  (`s06_cp12_geometry`)

**Question asked.** Given a closed system (finite labelled frozen CP-12 under closed unitary evolution, each subsystem's bath being the rest of the graph/matter/rotor system), does closure force an emergent spatial geometry or any geometry-selection law beyond known physics? Specifically: (a) can a loop-rich microscopic graph phase be assembled from the supplied preparations, and what initial resource does that require; (b) can CP-12's connection sector or matter response select or propagate geometry locally; (c) in the earlier Gaussian-network class (G1/GS1), do influence/access data alone reconstruct or select geometry?

**Assumptions / model class.**
- Frozen CP-12: finite labelled graph, N vertices, Q present edges, fermion hopping, positive rewiring Laplacian incl. diagonal, U(1) rotors with unbounded integer electric fields, exact Gauss constraints (cp12_preparation_minentropy_audit/REPORT.md:12; cp12_qn_degree2_ground_exclusion/REPORT.md:3)
- Frozen couplings (kappa,m,t,epsilon_E,Gamma)=(1/2,1,1,1/4,1/10) (cp12_qn_degree2_ground_exclusion/REPORT.md:3); checker hard-codes eps=.25, hop=1., gamma=.1 (check.py:39)
- External time parameter, Hamiltonian bounded below (GRUT_III/13_SYNTHESIS.md:137); closed unitary evolution rho_t=e^{-itH}rho_0e^{itH} (REPORT.md:12)
- Labelled counting; no permutation-gauge quotient (REPORT.md:3,103; 13_SYNTHESIS.md:111-113)
- Min-entropy extension: fixed density Q=nu N+O(1); target S = at least ceil(alpha N) vertex-disjoint cycles of microscopic length <=R, R,alpha,nu fixed; Pi_S graph-diagonal projector (REPORT.md:3,12,113)
- Main theorem preparation: flat incoherent neutral-sea mixture rho_0=(1/D) sum_G |G,0><G,0|, D=binom(M,Q), M=binom(N,2) (REPORT.md:7-12)
- General variant: (i) ||rho_0||_inf <= e^{cN}/binom(M,Q) AND (ii) <H-kappa Q>_0 = O(N) (REPORT.md:86-90); arbitrary-spectrum form uses H_inf(rho_0) with energy condition <H-kappa Q> <= cN called 'essential' (REPORT.md:113-119)
- Proof ingredients: unitary invariance of operator norm, Markov's inequality on F, energy conservation with m>=0, R>=0, T_hop>=-2|t_hop|Q, parent's labelled cycle counting (REPORT.md:31-52,64-68,104)
- Explicitly outside the min-entropy assumption: pure uniform coherent graph state and single pure graph state (REPORT.md:90,101); permutation-gauge quotient, open-system cooling, postselection, infinite-temperature thermodynamics, higher-density ground state (REPORT.md:103,159)
- Deficit Delta H_inf = log2 D_N - H_inf is relative to the neutral labelled graph subspace, not an absolute maximally mixed state; can be negative (REPORT.md:138-142)
- G1/GS1: small quadratic (Gaussian) static networks, supplied access structure, no gravity input (GS1:86-88,27-29; G1:105-110) — a different model class from CP-12, not cited in the handoff row

**Executed evidence.**
- Main all-time theorem for the flat incoherent mixture and its O(N^-delta) corollary; strict strengthening of parent O(1/log N) for this preparation only; does not improve the full D(rho_0||sigma_beta)=O(N) class — `research/reality_framework/cp12_preparation_minentropy_audit/REPORT.md:14-25` — sup_t Tr(rho_t Pi_S) <= (2|t_hop|+Gamma) Q N^{-(1+delta)} + exp[-(alpha - nu delta) N log N + O(N)], sup_t P_t(S) = O(N^{-delta}) for every delta < alpha/nu
- Representative exponents for prepared tori; finite-size bounds 'extremely loose'; 'asymptotic theorem, not a laboratory estimate' — `research/reality_framework/cp12_preparation_minentropy_audit/REPORT.md:27` — alpha=1/4, delta=1/16 for Q=2N, delta=1/24 for Q=3N
- Conserved maximum eigenvalue under closed unitary evolution (the only step where closure does work); energy control and Markov tail — `research/reality_framework/cp12_preparation_minentropy_audit/REPORT.md:31-52` — ||rho_t||_inf = D^{-1} for ALL times, <F>_t <= (2|t_hop|+Gamma) Q, Tr(rho_t P_>) <= (2|t_hop|+Gamma) Q / N^{1+delta}
- Arbitrary-spectrum bound and necessary min-entropy deficit threshold — `research/reality_framework/cp12_preparation_minentropy_audit/REPORT.md:122-155` — sup_T P_T(S_N) <= 2^{-H_inf(rho_0)} exp[(nu-alpha+nu delta) N ln N + O(N)] + C N^{-delta}, liminf Delta H_inf(N)/(N log2 N) >= alpha, roughly alpha N log2 N bits of deficit
- Two endpoints: incoherent mixture fails for all time; coherent pure equal superposition escapes 'without any proof that it assembles geometry'; 'coherence is not uniquely required' — `research/reality_framework/cp12_preparation_minentropy_audit/REPORT.md:157` — incoherent: H_inf = log2 D_N, deficit zero, coherent: H_inf = 0, deficit log2 D_N = nu N log2 N + O(N)
- Source self-grading: no new conceptual principle; sharpening of same proof, not a state-selection principle; Penrose resemblance not a derivation; closed unitary dynamics cannot generate missing spectral purity; open-system export/postselection/initial-condition theory outside the no-go — `research/reality_framework/cp12_preparation_minentropy_audit/REPORT.md:104,111,159`
- Checker executed in this session (python3 -I, isolated cwd): formula controls pass, but printed exponents show the explicit bound vacuous through N=10^18 — `research/reality_framework/cp12_preparation_minentropy_audit/check.py:18-56 (run output)` — exact packing 6 6 3 1 0.6883116883116883 parent_upper 1.0, exact packing 6 6 3 2 0.001998001998001998 parent_upper 1.0, nu 2 N 10^18 delta 0.0625 loglow/(NlogN) 0.206365 high_tail_upper 0.314956, nu 3 N 10^18 delta 0.041666666666666664 loglow/(NlogN) 0.251441 high_tail_upper 1.120316, CP12_PREPARATION_MINENTROPY_FORMULA_CHECK_PASS

**Answer.** All obstructions in this sector are model- and preparation-specific, each carrying a declared model (frozen labelled CP-12 at fixed couplings and Q/N) and a declared state class: the min-entropy no-go (flat incoherent mixture, or any state with ||rho_0||_inf <= e^{cN}/D AND O(N) excess energy, labelled counting, a specific loop-packing projector; REPORT.md:3,12,86-90,102-103); the parent thermal exclusion (nu>1, vacuous below N~10^7-10^8; 13_SYNTHESIS.md:301-302); the S_N marginal theorem (permutation-invariant preparations, unconditional labelled marginals; cp12_exchangeability_selection/REPORT.md:7-23); unicyclic/degree-two results (Q=N sector; cp12_holonomy_locality_gate/REPORT.md:17-37; cp12_qn_degree2_ground_exclusion/REPORT.md:7-13); matter-blind connection creation (uniform -Gamma/M; 14_CP12:30-37); the finite-size diameter trap (Q > binom(N-1,2); cp12_unmasked_formation/REPORT.md:19-27); and G1/GS1 (quadratic networks, supplied access; GS1:86-88; G1:105-110). The exact assumption class of the min-entropy extension is a min-entropy-plus-energy class, not a coherence class: (i) largest eigenvalue <= e^{cN}/binom(M,Q) (or any H_inf entering the sharpened bound) together with (ii) <H-kappa Q>_0 <= cN, the energy condition being 'essential' (REPORT.md:86-90,113-119). Pure coherent preparations are confirmed outside it: every pure state has H_inf=0; the source says a pure uniform coherent graph state and a single pure graph state fail (i) (REPORT.md:90), the theorem 'does not apply to the pure coherent neutral sea' (REPORT.md:101), and the coherent superposition 'escapes this necessary-condition obstruction, without any proof that it assembles geometry... coherence is not uniquely required' (REPORT.md:157). No theorem excludes every emergent geometry: the min-entropy theorem does not prohibit nonlocal, coarse-grained, relational, or growing-cycle-length space and makes no claim about gauge quotients, cooling, postselection or the higher-density ground state (REPORT.md:100,103); the S_N theorem does not forbid symmetric superpositions, infinite-volume SSB, or record-conditioned order (exchangeability:23); Theorem A does not exclude effective loop observables via superpositions or coarse-graining (holonomy:39); the degree-two exclusion is not an exclusion of all CP-12 phases (qn_degree2:13); the six-site propagation failure is 'not a no-go theorem for every CP-12 phase' (source_resolved:11-12). Conversely no packet claims a formed, persistent, dimension-selected spatial phase; the strongest positives are finite-N correlations, finite responses and prepared-host transport.

**Closure verdict.** Nothing additional was forced beyond known physics in the stated class, and the computation showing it is the min-entropy theorem itself. Closure enters at exactly one step: ||rho_t||_inf = ||rho_0||_inf for all t, the unitary invariance of the density-matrix spectrum (REPORT.md:31-35). With conserved extensive energy and labelled cycle counting (REPORT.md:37-82) it converts 'does frozen CP-12 assemble a microscopic loop-rich graph?' into 'which supplied initial state carries at least alpha N log2 N bits of min-entropy deficit relative to the flat neutral-graph mixture, with O(N) excess energy?' (REPORT.md:150-155). That is an initial-state obligation, not a state-selection law, as the source says three times (REPORT.md:104,111,159) and the partner audit confirms ('necessary resource... does not make low min-entropy sufficient', cp12_energy_matched_preparation/REPORT.md:21). What fixes the initial state remains supplied (cp12_unmasked_formation/REPORT.md:9), consistent with the handoff's own list of what closure does not force (NEXT_CHAT_HANDOFF.md:98-100). The explicit bound is moreover vacuous at every size its checker prints, through N=10^18 (REPORT.md:96; run output loglow/(NlogN)=+0.206365 at nu=2, +0.251441 at nu=3). Everything else here is a model-specific conditional theorem, a rejected mechanism, or recovered known physics (GS1:78-80; G1:38-39; 13_SYNTHESIS.md:84).

**Classification.** `mixed` — Conditional constraint: min-entropy/energy all-time bound and alpha N log2 N deficit threshold (REPORT.md:14-23,86-90,122-155); S_N marginal uniformity (exchangeability:13-20); unicyclic Lambda_R<=R/N (holonomy:11-37); degree-two ground exclusion (qn_degree2:7-13); extensive-circulation bounds (extensive_circulation:11-27); access-class split in Gaussian networks (GS1:15-29). Recovered known physics: proof ingredients (REPORT.md:35,104); counting obstruction prior art (13_SYNTHESIS.md:84-90); G1/GS1 machinery (GS1:78-80; G1:38-39). Supplied model choice: frozen Hamiltonian and couplings, Q/N sector, neutral ray / phase mask / recorded host, labelled counting (qn_degree2:3; energy_matched:9; unmasked_formation:9; 13_SYNTHESIS.md:111-113). Rejected mechanism: connection sector as local geometry source (14_CP12:30-37); recorded-ring metric (source_resolved:3-8); outside-core hopping suppression (dynamical_suppression:3-8); LocPos as selector (GS1:58-70); arrival-time functionals as metric (G1:60-77). Conjecture: Penrose/past-hypothesis resemblance (REPORT.md:159); relational geometry need not choose labels (exchangeability:23). Unexecuted task: Next Work test at Q=3N (REPORT.md:106); tensor-charter amendments (14_CP12:96-119); five-step relational gate (exchangeability:27-33); G1 second attack gated on owner ruling (G1:119-124).

### s07_wm_b1_b2 — WM / B1 / B2 matter and moving channels (preparation acceptance, recoverability, delivery, retention; B2 feasibility stop)  (`s07_wm_b1_b2`)

**Question asked.** In a finite closed quantum model whose bath for the matter subsystem is the rest of the same model (recorded geometry word, storage bits, tail registers and the retained 64N^2-mode conditional receiver, never reset), can one supplied time-independent Hamiltonian (i) start from an accepted prepared record, (ii) convert fuel to matter, (iii) deliver it to a readout region, (iv) retain it throughout a stated window, and (v) keep an encoded input recoverable — and does any of that force a law beyond ordinary quantum mechanics? Closeout sub-question: does any currently available quantitative route establish the moving-geometry thresholds (output >=1/10, retention >=1/4, conditional information error <=1/100) within the authorized compute envelope? (SPECIALIST_REVIEW.md:15-56, 85-88; MOVING_GEOMETRY.md:7-41; STOP_DECISION.md:6-13)

**Assumptions / model class.**
- Supplied law and constants: hard-core plus/minus species, three-level electric links with +sum E^2 and Gauss law, neutral fuel slots, 64N^2-mode bosonic receiver with nearest-mode element -1, g in [0.9,1.1], |bar_delta|<=1/2, |epsilon_i|<=1/4; jp=1/64, jm=1/96, A=13/46080, J=lambda=1/3840, Omega=2-2A (SPECIALIST_REVIEW.md:17-56; consolidation THEOREM.md:9-33)
- Recording, fuel loading and the accepted record are supplied, not autonomous formation claims (MOVING_GEOMETRY.md:41; SPECIALIST_REVIEW.md:60-70; P1 REPORT.md:3)
- Readout windows [L/(8J),L/(6J)] and [2/J,L/(6J)], decoder, and thresholds 1/10, 1/4, 1/100 are supplied (SPECIALIST_REVIEW.md:75-88)
- Every derivation is author-derived with independent mathematical review pending; the internal Challenger shares ancestry and is not independent review (REVIEW_STATUS.md:3-7; consolidation CHALLENGE.md:1-6; B1 CHALLENGE_AND_INTEGRATION.md:3-5)
- Closeout computational envelope: at most 25 s per child, 60 combined child seconds, 1 GiB memory, 25 MiB new files (FEASIBILITY.md:84-86)
- Preparation acceptance inherits S9's unreviewed threshold N>=2^28, L=64N^2, 4N^2<=t<=8N^2 => Pr(m>=ceil(N/2))>1/2 (wm2_physical_regime REPORT.md:7-11)
- Fixed-path family L in 24N, L>=2016 for B2-RA; whole-word moving family N>=2^28, L>=N/2 (FEASIBILITY.md:66-69; SPECIALIST_REVIEW.md:60-63)

**Answer.** Within the supplied-Hamiltonian class this sector established: an externally powered, conditional preparation (P1); a size-uniform refutation of autonomous every-time retention at one permitted parameter point (A1); a conditional one-composite delivery/retention window at author-derived status (B1); an author-derived fixed-path two-source pass (>12.852225% output, >43.4281% retention, exact conditional channel) for L in 24N, L>=2016 (B2-RA); exact reasons that pass does not transfer to the moving law (buffer hypothesis fails at t=0 with d_buffer=-1, witnesses J jp=1/245760 and J jm=1/368640, lost endpoint order identity, horizon 640L); an exact instrument reduction to Gram entries u,v,c,u_T,v_T (B2-IS); an optimal recovery formula plus a strict small-diagnostic obstruction to perfect four-dimensional recovery (MC-E1); and a documented stop because every available certificate (20L, 1411L, dense 2^66 bytes) is vacuous or out of envelope at N>=2^28. The moving-geometry operating question is unresolved: neither established nor refuted. The original eight-event B2 endpoint remains failed at 1.93%. The four notions stay distinct and are never multiplied: acceptance is conditional on unreviewed S9; recoverability is exact only conditionally (fixed path) or for a qutrit message; delivery and retention are proved only on the fixed path or the B1 one-composite window. The closeout is a feasibility stop within a stated envelope (1411/640 majorant, N=2^28, L=134,217,744, T=85,899,356,160, 2^62 modes, 2^66 bytes vs 2^30), explicitly not an impossibility proof. 'Incompatible state spaces not composable' is supported only in the specific forms the sources state (same-space comparison hypothesis fails; small diagnostics are not the physical space; one-resource channels do not tensorize); the sources leave a future same-space proof open.

**Closure verdict.** Nothing additional beyond known physics was forced in this class, and the sector's own computations show it. Every result is finite closed-system quantum mechanics applied to a supplied Hamiltonian with supplied preparation and readout: 'conventional quantum physics ... not an earned new law of nature' (A1 REPORT.md:118-120); 'The same leading predictions follow from a conventional tight-binding channel. Empirical distinction remains absent.' (B1 CHALLENGE_AND_INTEGRATION.md:19); 'No distinct empirical prediction follows.' (diagnosis ARCHITECTURAL_VERDICT.md:39); 'There is no new physical result' (REVIEW_STATUS.md:23). What closure (bath = rest of the model, never reset) did do was structural and computed, not postulated: (1) with no external sink, retention cannot be bought from the environment — A1's matter returns to fuel with total energy conserved (A1 REPORT.md:49-53) and B1's only surviving route is internal spatial transport in a finite window subject to recurrence (B1 FAILURE_MODES.md:36-39), i.e. known finite-system physics; (2) because the complement stays in the law, every operating estimate must control it, which is exactly why the fixed-path/buffer proof fails to compose (d_buffer=-1, MOVING_GEOMETRY.md:87-88) and why dense computation is out of envelope (2^62 modes, FEASIBILITY.md:72-75) — closure raised the proof and resource cost without supplying a new dynamical principle; (3) the information question reduces exactly to Gram entries of the closed evolution with a single SWAP primitive (B2-IS REPORT.md:36-44) and standard channel-fidelity formulas (MC-E1 REPORT.md:14). No GRUT-specific law, selection rule, prediction or gravity statement was earned here.

**Classification.** `mixed` — Recovered known physics: Rabi conversion, local perturbation bounds, bound-pair bands (A1 REPORT.md:118-120), tight-binding transport (B1 CHALLENGE_AND_INTEGRATION.md:19), finite recurrence (B1 FAILURE_MODES.md:36-39), optimal two-vector Gram recovery fidelity (MC-E1 REPORT.md:14-17). Conditional constraints: B2-RA bounds for L in 24N, L>=2016 (consolidation REPORT.md:16-26); P1 bound for N multiple of 64, N>=256 (P1 REPORT.md:46-52); B1 window for >=64 events (B1 REPORT.md:16-26); A1 refutation at Omega=13/4, lambda=1 (A1 REPORT.md:30-47); D1 regime (wm2_many_carrier REPORT.md:10-12). Supplied model choices: conversion law, fuel energy, contact, degree counterterm, color symmetry, recording, loading, decoder, thresholds (B1 REPORT.md:55-57; SPECIALIST_REVIEW.md:54-56,70; P1 REPORT.md:37-42). Rejected mechanisms: autonomous A1 retention (A1 REPORT.md:3-8); inherited empty-buffer composition for moving B2 (MOVING_GEOMETRY.md:85-95); gap-only two-level alternative (B1 FAILURE_MODES.md:3-15); dense enumeration within envelope (FEASIBILITY.md:72-75); positive-access and absolute-current routes as currently available (STOP_DECISION.md:17-23). Conjecture/unresolved: moving-geometry operation at N>=2^28 (STOP_DECISION.md:62); many-carrier theory (B1 REPORT.md:66). Unexecuted: independent review of every author-derived proof; Eq.(3) comparison (MOVING_GEOMETRY.md:178-183); bounds on u,v,c,u_T,v_T (SPECIALIST_REVIEW.md:92-101); specialist review packet prepared but 'no sending action was taken' (REVIEW_STATUS.md:24-25); six-event formation targets in S5/S6/S7/S8.

### s08_candidate1 — Candidate 1 scalar EFT (conventional Einstein/SM-plus-scalar candidate)  (`s08_candidate1`)

**Question asked.** Once a conventional closed law of this class (Einstein gravity + Standard Model with Dirac neutrinos + an added X/phi/D scalar sector) is fully specified and its charged organized object is computed classically, at one scalar loop, and under rotating fluctuations, does anything beyond known physics get forced? The packets' own framing was whether such a candidate could be the intended universal theory; their recorded outcome is "a narrower working model rather than the intended universal theory" (grut_candidate_1_20261018/REPORT.md:7), and the closure-fork reassessment states the loop coefficient "does not supply a new physical principle" (grut_closure_fork_20261019/REPORT.md:5-8).

**Assumptions / model class.**
- Seven supplied postulates P1-P7: time-oriented globally hyperbolic Lorentzian 4-manifold with spin structure; complex amplitudes, unitary perturbative EFT evolution, Born probabilities; SM + 3 nu_R + real phi, D and complex X; U(1)_X x Z2_phi x Z2_D and EFT B-L; Einstein action with reduced Planck mass; quartic potential with g_X^4 > lambda_phi lambda_X; explicit matching/preparation prescription (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:25-33). Dimension, orientation, gravity and Born rule are stated as supplied, not emergent (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:27-31; COMPATIBILITY.md:52; OPEN_CLAIMS.md:34).
- Action S_0 = int d^4x sqrt(-g)[M_P^2 R/2 - rho_Lambda + L_SM+nu_R - (1/2)(grad phi)^2 - |grad X|^2 - (1/2)(grad D)^2 - U] + S_boundary, with U = (lambda_phi/4)(phi^2-v^2)^2 + g_X^2 phi^2 |X|^2 + lambda_X |X|^4 + (1/2)(m_D0^2 + h_D^2 phi^2) D^2 + (lambda_D/4) D^4 (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:41-53).
- EFT completion rule: MSbar at mu_0 = g_X v; all symmetry-allowed counterterms admitted; renormalized xi's, m_X0^2, extra portals, curvature-squared and higher Wilson coefficients set to zero at mu_0 as matching choices that run; illustrative cutoff Lambda_EFT = 0.1 M_P (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:86-94).
- Supplied benchmark couplings g_X=.1, lambda_phi=.01, lambda_X=.001, h_D=.01, m_D0=g_X v, lambda_D=.001, v/M_P=10^-5, 'a declared illustrative point, not fitted or measured parameters of nature' (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:211; REPORT.md:35).
- Asymptotically flat benchmark with renormalized rho_Lambda=0, 'not a cosmological prediction' (REPORT.md:35; GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:55).
- Mechanism is admitted prior art: 'a quartic extension of the Friedberg-Lee-Sirlin/non-topological-soliton construction'; 'not an originality claim for GR, Q-balls, quantum mechanics, or scalar detectors' (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:217; stability REPORT.md:40).
- All quantum calculations are flat-space new-scalar loops only; SM and graviton loops excluded; SM sector sits in its vacuum (quantum REPORT.md:39; energy REPORT.md:51-52; COMPATIBILITY.md:22).
- The one-loop fixed-charge energy is conditional on stability of the reduced continuum saddle (energy REPORT.md:24-26; binding_results.json:77).

**Answer.** The action is Einstein-Hilbert with rho_Lambda, the full SM with Dirac neutrinos, and three gauge-singlet scalars X, phi, D with the quartic potential U, plus a GHY term and an explicit MSbar matching rule at mu_0 = g_X v (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:41-55, 86-94). Everything structural is supplied: spacetime, dimension, time orientation, Born probabilities, SM content, Einstein dynamics, new-sector couplings, zero matching values, rho_Lambda=0, the +v vacuum, the prepared charge and the ideal apparatus (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:25-33, 55, 64, 90, 193, 207, 211; OPEN_CLAIMS.md:33-34). Classically supported: a regular nodeless charged profile with E/(m_X Q)=0.91108652 at Q about 39,943.8462, tested local fixed-charge Hessian sectors, a resolved family with threshold and charge minimum, energetic closure of every two-lump and lump-plus-free partition drawn from the found envelope, finite-time (tau=40) 3D persistence, first-order Einstein response, and a weak reporter phase (REPORT.md:46-62; stability REPORT.md:46-51, 75-82, 111-129); the same law also has an adverse branch (-.02734 at Omega .97) and a second state above free carriers (stability REPORT.md:51, 55). The one-loop calculation shows, for flat-space new-scalar loops only and conditional on saddle stability, K_1/(gv)=2.79528 +/- 0.11 and a tree-plus-one-loop margin against 39944 free X carriers of -3553.70573 gv, range [-3553.81574, -3553.59573]; the loop moves the classical energy by .007681% (energy REPORT.md:9-26, 194-202). It does not show a lifetime, a residual-soliton-plus-emitted-X or multi-soliton quantum fission comparison, a continuum stability theorem, gravitational vacuum matching, higher-loop control, or empirical viability (energy REPORT.md:209-222; CHALLENGE.md:199-202); the three-charge one-loop family is unexecuted (NEXT_CALCULATION.md:1-3; README.md:60), and only its tree-level version was run, giving single-carrier gap -0.199999152115 gv and equal-split gap -3067.397336565 gv at N=39944 (closure fork REPORT.md:35-50). No novelty follows: the mechanism is FLS prior art, the spectral method is Graham's, lensing/Born outputs reproduce adopted laws, and the loop coefficient is a model-specific consistency number (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:217; closure fork REPORT.md:7-8, 20-26; PREDICTIONS_AND_TESTS.md:37, 49).

**Closure verdict.** Nothing additional was forced in the stated class, and the computation shows it. Every ingredient closure might have forced was instead supplied as a postulate or matching choice (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:25-33, 90, 211; OPEN_CLAIMS.md:34). The precise outputs (E/(m_X Q)=0.91108652; redshift 6.68813x10^-10; bending 2.89601x10^-10; delta_0(5gv)=0.0040665116; K_1/(gv)=2.79528; margin -3553.70573 gv; tree gaps -0.199999152115 and -3067.397336565 gv) are adopted laws applied to a supplied source or model-specific weak-coupling consistency numbers; the loop shifts the classical energy by .007681%, about a thousand times smaller than the classical binding fraction (PREDICTIONS_AND_TESTS.md:37; energy REPORT.md:202; closure fork REPORT.md:28-31). The one quantity bearing on a closed whole, the gravitating total vacuum energy, is not fixed by the model: the scalar loop shifts it to -1.22652632365x10^-6 v^4, "No adjustment has been applied here," and quantum asymptotic flatness needs an explicit total-vacuum-energy matching condition that closure did not supply (quantum REPORT.md:7, 128-138). The closure-fork assessment recommends status C "with Candidate 1 preserved as a numerical EFT benchmark" (closure fork REPORT.md:153-154). This sector contributes a worked negative to the parent question.

**Classification.** `mixed` — Recovered known physics: Einstein weak-field response, Born-rule readout, FLS-type charge-stabilized soliton, exact one-loop RG closure of a renormalizable scalar polynomial (PREDICTIONS_AND_TESTS.md:37; GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:217; quantum REPORT.md:53-58). Conditional constraint: one-loop free-carrier binding at the benchmark, conditional on reduced-saddle stability and the +/-0.11 allowance; tree-order closure of single-emission and equal-split channels at N=39944 (energy REPORT.md:19-26; closure fork REPORT.md:37-50). Supplied model choice: all couplings, matching zeros, rho_Lambda=0, prepared charge, apparatus (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:90, 211). Rejected mechanism: the "coefficients vanish at every scale" rule and the pointwise Coleman-Weinberg integral shortcut (quantum REPORT.md:67, 124). Unexecuted task: three-charge one-loop family, lifetime, nonlinear gravity, apparatus, empirical calibration (NEXT_CALCULATION.md:1-3; OPEN_CLAIMS.md:21-34). The dominant closure-relevant finding is a supplied-model-choice negative: nothing forced.

**Remaining freedom.**
- New-sector couplings v, g_X, lambda_phi, lambda_X, m_D0, h_D, lambda_D and v/M_P are a declared illustrative point (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:211).
- Renormalized xi's, m_X0^2, extra portals, curvature-squared and higher Wilson coefficients are zero at mu_0 by choice and run away from it (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:90; quantum REPORT.md:63-67, 73-79).
- rho_Lambda, the polynomial constant C and the total vacuum-energy matching are free; the benchmark rho_Lambda=0 is bookkeeping, not prediction (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:55; quantum REPORT.md:134).
- Choice of the +v asymptotic vacuum is boundary/preparation data; domain walls allowed but outside the regime (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:64).
- Prepared charge/state, apparatus state sigma_A, reference phase, foliation and time orientation are supplied (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:151, 193, 207).
- SM content, theta=0, Dirac neutrinos and Lambda_EFT=0.1 M_P are imported or illustrative choices (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:82, 92).
- A different potential is permitted only as an explicitly named Candidate 2 (OPEN_CLAIMS.md:54).

**Failures retained.**
- Six failed preliminary solver starts retained (REPORT.md:44; OPEN_CLAIMS.md:39).
- Turning-point minimizer reached its call cap; all twelve first finite-volume optimizations failed their gradient tolerance (stability REPORT.md:44, 69).
- Negative fixed-charge radial Hessian -.02734 at Omega=.97; second state at reference charge with Ebar about 400.00816 above free carriers (stability REPORT.md:51, 55).
- Equal three-, four-, five-lump partitions unresolved, not forbidden (stability REPORT.md:86).
- Strong-strain run: persistent deformed state, core charge still declining at last sample; not return to spherical profile (stability REPORT.md:127-129).
- Narrower 'vanish at every scale' rule rejected (quantum REPORT.md:67); static Hessian negative at 84/1001 radii, min h_a=-0.00357857280730 v^2 (quantum REPORT.md:115-119); scalar vacuum energy not zero after loops, no adjustment applied, conditional AdS consequence L_AdS v about 1.56x10^8 (quantum REPORT.md:98, 128-136).
- Preserved ill-conditioned phase-Jordan pair s about +/-3.40x10^-7; uncertified half-line bound; sparse probes do not exhaust spectra (fluctuations REPORT.md:84, 97, 139).
- Stricter replay changed the remainder by +.00851301935, showing the initial 6/8 quadrature agreement was not a complete error estimate; +/-0.11 is not a certified enclosure (energy REPORT.md:19-24, 120-126).
- Residual-soliton emission and multi-soliton fission 'Not calculated at one loop here'; lifetime 'Not calculated' (energy REPORT.md:216-219).
- Three-charge one-loop family in NEXT_CALCULATION.md unexecuted (NEXT_CALCULATION.md:1-3; README.md:60); closure fork ran tree order only and computed no new Q-ball loop spectrum or quantum lifetime (closure fork REPORT.md:37, 178-179).
- Su-Xie-Zhou caution: classical and quantum-corrected FLS stability can differ; 'Our quantum lifetime remains unknown' (OPEN_CLAIMS.md:50).
- Internal challenge is not independent review; challenger did not audit the completed spectral output (REPORT.md:11; COMPATIBILITY.md:60; energy REPORT.md:239-243; CHALLENGE.md:167-171).

**Must not be promoted.**
- E/(m_X Q)=0.91108652, -3553.70573 gv, +2.79528 gv, -0.199999152115 gv and -3067.397336565 gv are benchmark-specific numbers at supplied couplings, not universal predictions (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:211; energy REPORT.md:225-228; closure fork results.json:143).
- The tree gaps must not be called quantum channel results (closure fork DERIVATION.md:41-43).
- Check counts (25, 60, 290, 39, 42, 90, 38) are executed-check counts, not physical validation (REPORT.md:80; stability REPORT.md:153; quantum REPORT.md:157; fluctuations REPORT.md:183; VERIFICATION.json:205; closure fork REPORT.md:31-33).
- rho_Lambda=0 is not a prediction of a vanishing cosmological constant; L_AdS v about 1.56x10^8 is a conditional consequence, not a prediction (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:55; quantum REPORT.md:136).
- 'Charge-stabilized' and 'stable' must keep the tested energetic/linear/finite-time meaning; no lifetime exists (COMPATIBILITY.md:44; stability REPORT.md:157).
- Lensing, redshift and Born probabilities are adopted laws applied to a source, not GRUT-distinct predictions (PREDICTIONS_AND_TESTS.md:37, 49).
- The phi profile is not memory; no D1/B1/B2/MC-E1 theorem transfers (OPEN_CLAIMS.md:38, 40; REPORT.md:68).
- Small compactness is not all-charge stability; thin-wall extrapolation to large charge is prohibited (OPEN_CLAIMS.md:47; PREDICTIONS_AND_TESTS.md:74).
- The one-loop margin's +/-0.11 allowance is not a bound on two-loop, gravitational, nonperturbative or empirical errors (energy REPORT.md:166-167; binding_protocol.json:11).

**Handoff-row discrepancies found.**
- Order mislabel (NEXT_CHAT_HANDOFF.md:164-166): the handoff calls -3554 gv a 'classical free-carrier binding margin'. The source prints -3553.70573404 as 'Difference, soliton minus free carriers' in the same-order tree-plus-one-loop table (soliton energy including K_1 minus N times the strict one-loop pole mass) (energy REPORT.md:194-201; binding_results.json:56). The purely classical margin from the same sources is about -3551.58 gv at N=39944 (36392.42286136366 - 39944 with mX/(gv)=1, closure fork DERIVATION.md:31-35; my arithmetic) or +35.51546387 Ebar = 3551.546 gv at the continuous reference charge (stability REPORT.md:77). The number is right for the one-loop comparison; the label is wrong. The 2.13 gv difference equals the pole-threshold shift (39948.92387308 - 39944) minus the loop shift 2.79528.
- Omitted qualifiers (NEXT_CHAT_HANDOFF.md:164): '+2.80 gv' rounds 2.79528 but drops the stated +/-0.11 allowance, the range [2.68528, 2.90528], and the explicit conditionality on reduced-saddle stability (energy REPORT.md:19-26, 163).
- Provenance (NEXT_CHAT_HANDOFF.md:167-168): the single-carrier gap -.199999152115 gv and equal-split gap -3067.397336565 gv are not printed in any grut_candidate_1_* packet; they exist only in grut_closure_fork_20261019 (REPORT.md:41-42; DERIVATION.md:39-40; results.json:137-138). The row does link the closure fork, so this is a provenance note. The stability packet's earlier equal-split excess at the non-integer reference charge is +30.67384256 Ebar = 3067.384 gv (stability REPORT.md:80), a distinct number consistent to about 0.013 gv.
- Row omission (NEXT_CHAT_HANDOFF.md:121): 'classically supported charged objects' is correct but does not mention the adverse branch (-.02734 at Omega .97) or the second, higher-energy state at reference charge (stability REPORT.md:51, 55).
- No discrepancy found in 'specified action', 'model-specific one-loop calculation', 'conditional on supplied couplings', 'no novelty or validated universe follows', or 'supports the specified channel ... not lifetime or all quantum fragmentation' (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:41-53, 211, 217; closure fork REPORT.md:5-8; energy REPORT.md:28-30, 216-219).

### s09_closure_noboundary — Closure / no-boundary (C1, C2, B three-prescription control, Obadia coefficient, curvature sign)  (`s09_closure_noboundary`)

**Question asked.** Does the closure premise ("one closed whole", "nothing outside", observer bath = commutant), read either as C1 ("no boundary data of any kind") or C2 (GRUT III P1 observer restriction), force beyond adopted GR/QFT and a named state prescription any of: (i) a cosmological state or lapse-integration-cycle selection rule; (ii) a new thermality result for a comoving observer in slow roll (the proposed D2 claim built on T_eff=H(1-epsilon)/(2 pi)); (iii) a sign or magnitude of present spatial curvature usable as a falsifier? Follow-up: does 0b6df6f contain a stronger hidden closure condition, and is the B three-prescription control executed? (REPORT.md:1-16, :145-166; CLOSURE_SELECTION_RESPONSE.md:3-12, :157-162; protocol.json:2; AGENT_COORDINATION.md:5796, :5802)

**Assumptions / model class.**
- Thermality check: free massless conformally coupled scalar, conformal vacuum, spatially flat FLRW, comoving worldline, fixed mean time, H(0)=a(0)=1, epsilon=-Hdot/H^2 small with the rest of the hierarchy small; exact test uses constant-epsilon power law a=(1+epsilon t)^(1/epsilon) (DERIVATION.md:50-62, :90-92; protocol.json:5; probe.py:55-64)
- Curvature checks: k=+1 FLRW, Omega_K=-1/(a^2 H^2), c=1; closed global de Sitter a=cosh(H_Lambda t)/H_Lambda continued from a regular Euclidean hemisphere; flat three-torus for the topology point (DERIVATION.md:136-152, :167-171)
- Temperature-transfer check IMPORTS the Gibbons-Hawking geodesic temperature H_Lambda/(2 pi); not rederived (curvature_transfer_protocol.json:4; DERIVATION.md:177-180; probe.py:117)
- Sequestering remark assumes the ORIGINAL global equation MP^2 Gmunu=Tmunu-(1/4)gmunu<T>, homogeneous FLRW, finite integrals and vanishing endpoint term (DERIVATION.md:199-209)
- C2/P1 analysis uses archived GRUT III P1 as written: IDENTIFICATION, credit 0, triple (A(O), omega|_A(O), sigma^omega_t), bath = commutant A(O)' (= A(O') where Haag duality holds), type III_1 algebras, no tensor factorisation, no influence functional, no dynamics (GRUT_III/01_PRINCIPLES.md:23-43, :81)
- Arithmetic: Python 3.12.14, NumPy 2.3.5, SciPy 1.17.0, 60-digit Decimal; no package installed; probe.py not rerun for this entry, results.json taken as recorded (README.md:20-23; results.json key versions)
- Registered B control model class: closed minisuperspace ds^2=-N^2 dt^2/q + q dOmega_3^2, 8 pi G=1, H=1, Lambda=3, hbar=1; real massless minimally coupled SPECTATOR scalar mode (not an inflaton curvature perturbation); quadratic mode action on background saddles; leading semiclassical lapse saddle; no backreaction or prefactor correction (grut_closure_control_20261019/protocol.json:6-14)
- Checkout: task says 96a7e7e; actual HEAD is da8f925 (WIP checkpoint descendant). git diff --stat 96a7e7e HEAD over both sector packets and the handoff directory is empty, so cited content is identical at the handoff commit

Modular KMS is automatic for a faithful normal state and sets no physical-time temperature. Ivo-Li-Maldacena trace-saddle matching is already part of the published prescription, with normalization/selection left open. Verdict: 'C2 identifies the appropriate observer-state object, but P1 does not currently add a state-selection condition.
