**Answer.** (1) C2 does not supply a state-selection rule: restriction to an observer algebra exists for every global state, modular KMS is automatic and sets no physical-clock temperature, and the Ivo–Li–Maldacena trace-saddle matching is already part of the published prescription; the second reading of C2 (no global state) would be a new construction still lacking a local-state rule and overlap compatibility. No stronger hidden closure condition exists in 0b6df6f (CLOSURE_SELECTION_RESPONSE.md:7-12, :28-55, :84-88, :169-171). C1 ("no boundary data of any kind") does not determine a lapse cycle; if defined as prescription (a) its inverse-Gaussian failure terminates only that definition (CLOSURE_SELECTION_RESPONSE.md:157-162). (2) The slow-roll coefficient T_eff=H(1-epsilon)/(2 pi) is not a new thermality result: it is the first-order term of Obadia 2008 eqs. (20)-(22) and an already-recorded v11 theorem in flat FLRW; the packet's executed additions (second-order residual s^2/4 - s^3 coth(s/2)/12, endpoint term (s/2)[2 - s coth(s/2)], first-order non-stationarity) follow from the adopted conformal vacuum and show midpoint thermality is neither a global KMS state nor a detector result; D2 is withdrawn, no novelty certificate (REPORT.md:71-76; DERIVATION.md:50-132; CLOSURE_SELECTION_RESPONSE.md:106-111; results.json:193-203, :367-377). (3) Curvature sign is not a discriminator: compactness does not imply k=+1 (flat torus, Ricci scalar 0), and k=+1 supplies no lower bound on |Omega_K| (closed de Sitter: -4.54e-5 at N=5, -2.06e-9 at N=10, -7.67e-53 at N=60), so a finite flatness bound cannot exclude the sign class; a distribution over N with reheating/history is a prescription choice. The flat thermal formula also fails on the closed saddle by sqrt(1+|Omega_K|) (REPORT.md:93-118; DERIVATION.md:134-195; results.json:382-445). The only positive-curvature derivation is conditional on original global sequestering with finite integrals and vanishing endpoint term (DERIVATION.md:199-215). (4) The B three-prescription control is registered and unexecuted: pre-registered model, benchmark q1=40, ell=2, b=1.5, three published prescriptions, grids, acceptance and decision rules; it would reproduce published spectator-mode Gaussian widths, which the protocol itself classes as established physics, not a GRUT prediction; no contour or perturbation code was run; Steps 2–3 not performed (grut_closure_control_20261019/protocol.json:1-47; STATUS.md:1-10). Recorded program status: option C, preserve the map (REPORT.md:145-166).

**Closure verdict.** Nothing additional was forced in the stated class. The computations/arguments that show it: (i) the restriction map exists for every state on the encompassing algebra, so observer-restriction (C2/P1) selects no state and fixes no one-point function (CLOSURE_SELECTION_RESPONSE.md:28-40); (ii) the first-order temperature shift is recovered known physics (Obadia 2008 eqs. 20-22; archived v11), and the executed finite-separation checks — second-order residual coefficient s^2/4 - s^3 coth(s/2)/12 matched to <2% at s=0.5,1,2,3; endpoint first-order term (s/2)[2 - s coth(s/2)] matched to <2%; first-order non-stationarity under translation — follow from the adopted conformal vacuum in flat FLRW, not from closure (DERIVATION.md:50-132; results.json:193-203, :367-377); (iii) the exact closed-de-Sitter identity Omega_K=-1/(e^{2N}-1) (-4.5401991009687765e-05 at N=5, -2.061153626686912e-09 at N=10) and the flat torus (Ricci scalar 0) show closure/compactness force neither the curvature sign nor a magnitude (results.json:382-430); (iv) the exactly soluble closed case shows the flat temperature formula is off by sqrt(1+|Omega_K|), with discrepancy/|Omega_K| -> 0.5 (0.49999432487995393 at N=5) (results.json:389-423); (v) the only positive-curvature derivation found is conditional on the original global sequestering equation with finite integrals and vanishing endpoint term — a supplied global modification, not closure (DERIVATION.md:199-215). The sources add that this is not a proof that no future explicit postulate could have content: 'This does not declare the program permanently impossible. It identifies the missing mathematical choice that another construction would have to resolve' (REPORT.md:161-166; grut_closure_control_20261019/protocol.json:44).

**Classification.** `mixed` — recovered known physics: T_eff=H(1-epsilon)/(2 pi) (Obadia 2008; v11 flat theorem), Gibbons-Hawking H_Lambda/(2 pi) (imported), closed-de-Sitter Omega_K identity, Ivo-Li-Maldacena trace matching, restriction/modular-KMS facts (CLOSURE_SELECTION_RESPONSE.md:42-47, :64-71, :97-105; DERIVATION.md:177-180). conditional constraint: original global sequestering <R>=0 => k int a dt > 0 under finite integrals and vanishing endpoint term (DERIVATION.md:199-209); v11 first-order thermality only for a conformal field in flat FLRW at fixed mean time (DERIVATION.md:101-105). supplied model choice: action, state prescription, integration cycle, measure, conditioning, N-distribution, reheating (NEXT_QUESTION.md:6-39). rejected mechanism: sign-only curvature falsifier; 'all sequestering forces k=+1'; D2 novelty; flat-formula transfer to a closed saddle; C1 == prescription (a) as a closure consequence (CLOSURE_SELECTION_RESPONSE.md:157-171; DERIVATION.md:192-195). conjecture: that some explicit closure condition could select a state/cycle (REPORT.md:155-159; NEXT_QUESTION.md:48-51). unexecuted task: B Step 1 three-prescription control and Steps 2-3 (grut_closure_control_20261019/STATUS.md:1-10; CLOSURE_SELECTION_RESPONSE.md:172-174).

**Remaining freedom.**
- Choice of action and matter content: ordinary Einstein/inflaton vs an explicit global modification such as sequestering; a no-boundary wavefunction for one action cannot be combined with another action's constraints without recalculation (NEXT_QUESTION.md:6-10)
- The state datum: integration cycle C, boundary conditions, allowed topologies, measure, perturbative domain — 'The phrase "one closed whole" does not by itself specify this datum' (NEXT_QUESTION.md:12-22)
- Whether any closure condition selects among viable cycles/states without using the desired final spectrum or curvature to make the choice (NEXT_QUESTION.md:24-31)
- The N-distribution, potential, reheating/history and conditioning needed to turn a curvature sign into any present numerical prediction (NEXT_QUESTION.md:33-39; DERIVATION.md:161-166)
- Closed-geometry corrections to the thermal bridge, what a finite-duration detector measures, and priority of the finite-separation/minimally coupled lemma (NEXT_QUESTION.md:41-46; SOURCES.md:22-25)

- The B Step 1 outcome itself (pass/partial/failure) and hence Steps 2-3 (grut_closure_control_20261019/protocol.json:40-45)
- The FLT vs Diaz Dorronsoro contour dispute, explicitly not resolved (SOURCES.md:17)

### M1 memory (key s10_m1_memory): finite Ising repetition register with matter feedback and programmed flying-qubit writer; facilitation benefit withdrawn  (`s10_m1_memory`)

**Question asked.** Can one closed, time-independent Hamiltonian with no external bath, drive, disorder or controller (a) retain a readable redundantly encoded classical record while the record alters later matter interactions and matter information spreads, (b) write such a record autonomously from internal resources, and (c) is a proposed extra law, state-dependent kinetic saturation (facilitation), what protects the record? Under the parent closed-whole question: does closure force a new memory principle here, or does the record persist for a reason known physics already contains? (pkt/protocol.json:5-8,21; pkt/writer_protocol.json:11; pkt/REPORT.md:44-47)

**Assumptions / model class.**
- Ring of L>=3 cells, each a matter qubit sigma_i and a record qubit tau_i; writer adds L^2(R,dQ) x C^2_R for a massive carrier with internal source bit (pkt/GRUT_CANDIDATE_M1.md:17-32)
- Core law H_M1 = sum_i [J(1+alpha tau_i^z) sigma_i^z sigma_{i+1}^z + h_x sigma_i^x + h_z sigma_i^z - K tau_i^z tau_{i+1}^z - Delta F_i tau_i^x], F_i = eps + (1-eps)(1 - tau_{i-1}^z tau_{i+1}^z)/2; writer H = P^2/(2M) + H_M1 + Omega(1-R^z)/2 sum_i f_i(Q) tau_i^x with fixed cos^2 gates (pkt/GRUT_CANDIDATE_M1.md:34-66)
- Benchmark hbar=J=1, (alpha,h_x,h_z,K,Delta,eps)=(0.5,1.05,0.5,1,1,0.05); writer L=4, M=0.5, p0=100, q0=-4, sigma_Q=0.35, w=0.3, Omega=pi(p0/M)/(2w)=1047.1975511965977 (pkt/GRUT_CANDIDATE_M1.md:93-101; pkt/writer_results_initial.json parameters.Omega)
- Supplied postulates: quantum mechanics, fixed local substrate, kinetic-saturation projector, physical feedback via alpha, modeled preparation resources; eps and feedback are supplied, not derived from 'memory' or 'closure' (pkt/GRUT_CANDIDATE_M1.md:49-51,75-89)
- External Hamiltonian time fundamental; no gauge or gravitational constraint (pkt/GRUT_CANDIDATE_M1.md:29-32)
- Pure prepared states with nonzero excitation energy, not a ground state or finite-temperature phase; sizes L=3,4,5 only; retention grid [0,40] step 0.2 (201 points), readout [0,2] step 0.02 (pkt/WORKED_REGIME.md:124-125; pkt/protocol.json:32; pkt/calculate.py:110-111)
- Success observable is equal-prior local guessing P_A = 1/2 + |<A>_+ - <A>_-|/4, not optimal global recovery (pkt/GRUT_CANDIDATE_M1.md:133-141)
- Matched control replaces only F_i by constant eps=0.05, everything else fixed; specified after the review outcome was known, 'not a blinded or held-out prediction' (pkt/REPORT.md:18-20; pkt/control_protocol.json:6-7)
- Core dynamics closed: no quenched disorder or external controller; equilibrium diagnostic has no external bath; writer has no external pulse, reset, postselection or reservoir (pkt/protocol.json:8,21; pkt/writer_protocol.json:11)

**Executed evidence.**
- Core M1 retention/readout at L=3,4,5 (all 13 registered conditions pass; seven original runs 11.79 s) — `pkt/WORKED_REGIME.md:75-79; pkt/EXECUTION.md:11-16; pkt/summary.json core[0..2]; pkt/results_initial.json all_registered_acceptance_pass` — L=3: 0.994999 / 0.985006 / readout 0.806447 at t=1.20, L=4: 0.987510 / 0.935065 / readout 0.778468 at t=0.58 (full: 0.9875095925556739, 0.9350645853495996, 0.7784678210302141), L=5: 0.989132 / 0.940399 / readout 0.778460, L=4 P_Y = 1/2 + |-0.491561535 - 0.622309749|/4 = 0.778467821; memory at readout 0.999312115 (pkt/WORKED_REGIME.md:87-95)
- Matched uniform-floor control F=eps=0.05 retains better at all sizes, nearly equal readout; uniform no worse at all 201 sampled times within 1e-10; executed by review tensor script and packet bit-table (ten-run replay 7.31415 s) — `pkt/REPORT.md:22-29; pkt/WORKED_REGIME.md:83-85,111-114; rev/control_results.txt:1-6; rev/REVIEW.md:25-36; pkt/summary.json matched_comparison; pkt/calculate.py:110,301; pkt/EXECUTION.md:30-46` — L=3: M1 min t<=10 0.994999 vs 0.998738; t=40 0.985006 vs 0.994692; min[0,40] 0.984591 vs 0.994266; readout 0.806447 vs 0.806626, L=4: 0.987510 vs 0.999055; 0.935065 vs 0.998458; min[0,40] 0.935065 vs 0.998397; readout 0.778468 vs 0.778483 (full 0.7784678210302141 vs 0.7784834017608981 at 0.58; diff 1.5580730684083832e-05), L=5: 0.989132 vs 0.998479; 0.940399 vs 0.997517; min[0,40] 0.940399 vs 0.995717; readout 0.778460 vs 0.778475, best-readout differences 0.000179931, 0.000015581, 0.000015652 (pkt/EXECUTION.md:46), raw min(uniform - candidate): -2.7755575615628914e-15 (L=3), 8.881784197001252e-16 (L=4), -1.7763568394002505e-15 (L=5); tolerance test delta.min() >= -1e-10, mechanism_claims: facilitation_improves_minimum_retention_all_sizes=false; matched_uniform_control_retains_better_on_registered_samples=true

- Six registered stronger attacks fail and remain false (three numerical-method checks true) — `pkt/WORKED_REGIME.md:150-165; pkt/challenge_results.json checks, local_bit, autonomous_write, unfacilitated_perturbations; pkt/summary.json adversarial_conditions; pkt/challenge_protocol.json:2` — single-cell at t=10: 0.801481 (L=4), 0.751472 (L=5), bare write maxima 0.553352 (L=4), 0.526376 (L=5), kappa=0.1 min t<=10 0.875748; kappa=0.3 0.764940, challenge run 7.36 s; independent_full_H_max_error 4.163336342344337e-17
- Writer M1-W: impulsive pi/2 controlled flip; per-cell recovery, storage, readout, refinement, energy accounting, diagnostics — `pkt/REPORT.md:75-78,84-95,116-128; pkt/COMPATIBILITY.md:34-39; rev/REVIEW.md:42-44; pkt/writer_results_refined.json runs[*]; pkt/summary.json writer_minimum_memory_t10, writer_refinement; pkt/EXECUTION.md:64-78` — gate crossing 2w/(p0/M) = 0.003; pulse area Omega w/(p0/M) = pi/2, per-cell at t=0.06: at least 0.999680 (derived min cell 0.999680152 / 0.999680089 / 0.999680260 in the three runs), storage all-cell through t10 >= 0.991697 (0.9916972267188419 refined; 0.9916969879289492 coarse), readout 0.769977 at storage t=0.52, memory 0.998568 (0.769977434073726 / 0.9985675362234426), max probability change across refinements < 6.8e-7 (6.705103664517154e-07), carrier 10002.0408163 -> 10004.0290991; core 1.55 -> -0.437569946; total drift 0.000712816 J (per run 0.00010979246144415811, 1.0308212949894369e-08, 0.0007128160505089909: nonmonotonic), norm error 1.33e-12; gate residual 6.87e-10; edge 1.76e-9; negative momentum 1.36e-8, coarse run 414.59 s; replay 314.89 s; dt=0.0001 local-Pauli replay interrupted (Ctrl-C) and superseded
- Numerical integrity, exact replay, finalizer — `pkt/WORKED_REGIME.md:129-133; pkt/MANIFEST.json original_replay_max_conditional_mean_error, files; pkt/verify.py:46-52,63-71,84-95; read-only sha256 comparison performed here` — norm deviation 1.29e-14; energy deviation < 3.07e-14 J; Kronecker/Krylov 2.24e-13 (retention), 5.2e-15 (readout means), original_replay_max_conditional_mean_error = 0.0 (assert < 1e-10), 31 manifest files plus MANIFEST.json; 31/31 sha256+size match at HEAD; review rows reproduced within 5.01e-7
- Equilibrium and scrambling diagnostics; no exponent — `pkt/WORKED_REGIME.md:177-190; pkt/summary.json lyapunov_exponent, gravitational_prediction; pkt/challenge_results.json centered_regulated_otoc` — beta=1 memory autocorrelation 0.538351 at t=10; plateau 0.458812, adjacent matter squared commutator reaches 1.41102 in [0,4], centered factorization defect 0.607085; lyapunov_exponent = null; gravitational_prediction = null
- Headline change from 1997d20 to 4e9a161 — `git show 1997d20:pkt/REPORT.md lines 1-16; 1997d20:pkt/OPEN_CLAIMS.md lines 48-53; pkt/OPEN_CLAIMS.md:56-61; AGENT_COORDINATION.md:5806-5814,5816-5822` — original: 'a constructed and calculated memory candidate', added principle 'kinetic saturation', classification 'narrower working model rather than the intended universal theory', corrected: 'finite Ising repetition register with matter feedback and a programmed flying-qubit writer; the proposed facilitation benefit is rejected in the demonstrated regime'
- Secondary cross-check at HEAD (not a primary source): unchanged review script rerun, output identical to control_results.txt — `research/reality_framework/grut_closed_whole_theory_20261019/results/m1_matched_control_rerun.txt:1-12` — Python 3.13.16, numpy 2.5.3, scipy 1.18.1, wall 8.6 s; diff empty

**Answer.** What works in the stated finite regime: a prepared ferromagnetic repetition register (L=3-5, one classical bit repeated) retains the bit at >=0.987510 through sampled t=10 and 0.935065 at t=40 (L=4), while a tau-dependent matter bond yields a 0.778468 local matter readout; at eps=0 retention is exact by domain-wall conservation; a closed time-independent flying-qubit writer copies a source bit into all four cells at >=0.999680 with the register's energy change paid by the carrier (pkt/REPORT.md:84-95,116-119; pkt/WORKED_REGIME.md:13-44). What was withdrawn: the attribution of that retention to the facilitation (kinetic-saturation) law. The matched F=eps control retains better at every sampled time and size (L=4, t=40: 0.998458 vs 0.935065) with nearly the same readout (0.778483 vs 0.778468), so 'the demonstrated retention therefore does not support the added facilitation principle. The finite calculation remains correct; the attribution of its protection to that principle is withdrawn' (pkt/REPORT.md:3-8; pkt/OPEN_CLAIMS.md:16-19). The reviewer's mechanism: memory is the ferromagnetic gap of a T=0 transverse-field Ising repetition code; facilitation is inactive on an ordered record and converts a single leak into resonant East-model spreading (rev/REVIEW.md:36). Not established: a finite-temperature memory phase (pkt/REPORT.md:39-40; pkt/COMPATIBILITY.md:41-42; reviewer's Peierls remark rev/REVIEW.md:48 is not a packet computation); cloning of an unknown quantum state (pkt/GRUT_CANDIDATE_M1.md:143-144); gravity (pkt/COMPATIBILITY.md:8; pkt/REPORT.md:155-167); a chaos exponent (pkt/REPORT.md:159-161; summary.json lyapunov_exponent null); all-times retention or scattering certificate (pkt/REPORT.md:29,111-112,126-128); independent local capacity, thermodynamic localization, robustness to thermal inclusions (pkt/OPEN_CLAIMS.md:38-40); autonomous formation from generic resources (pkt/OPEN_CLAIMS.md:36-37); novelty or empirical viability (pkt/OPEN_CLAIMS.md:50-52).

**Closure verdict.** Nothing additional was forced beyond known physics in the stated class; the computation that shows it is the matched-control table (L=3: 0.994999 vs 0.998738 min through t=10 and 0.985006 vs 0.994692 at t=40; L=4: 0.987510 vs 0.999055 and 0.935065 vs 0.998458; L=5: 0.989132 vs 0.998479 and 0.940399 vs 0.997517; pkt/REPORT.md:22-26). The law was specified closed (no external bath, reservoir, pulse or postselection: pkt/protocol.json:8,21; pkt/writer_protocol.json:11; pkt/COMPATIBILITY.md:14-17), and within it the record persists for a reason known physics already contains, the gap of -K tau^z tau^z against a weak transverse field, i.e. a T=0 transverse-field Ising repetition code (rev/REVIEW.md:36; pkt/REPORT.md:38-39), while the one proposed extra law makes retention worse. What closure did supply is standard quantum-mechanical bookkeeping: energy conservation makes the carrier pay the register's energy change (core 1.55 -> -0.437569946 as carrier 10002.0408163 -> 10004.0290991; pkt/REPORT.md:116-118; rev/REVIEW.md:42); the nonselective readout removes -1.2010144303 and -0.8997375490 from the modeled system, which an energy-conserving detector completion must receive and which is not modeled (pkt/WORKED_REGIME.md:143-148); and the conserved domain-wall number at eps=0 forbids gaining weight in the protected sector, so 'perfect protection is not a mechanism for generating the protected state' (pkt/WORKED_REGIME.md:41-44). The packet states that closure plus structured memory 'does not by itself bound the largest exponent of the other operators' and leaves an eikonal G_N s, s ~ e^{2 pi T t} channel untouched (pkt/COMPATIBILITY.md:60-77). Result: a finite working construction in known physics with the proposed addition rejected by its own numbers (pkt/REPORT.md:169-176).

**Classification.** `mixed` — Recovered known physics: repetition-bit retention by a ferromagnetic gap against a weak transverse field; pi/2 pulse-area controlled-flip writer; energy conservation in closed scattering; exact conservation of D at eps=0 (rev/REVIEW.md:36,42; pkt/WORKED_REGIME.md:13-22). Rejected mechanism: the facilitation-benefit interpretation of kinetic saturation, rejected by the matched F=eps control at L=3,4,5 (pkt/REPORT.md:137-139; pkt/summary.json mechanism_claims). Supplied model choice: ring topology, two qubits per cell, projector form of F_i, eps=0.05, alpha=0.5, parameter point, writer apparatus parameters, local guessing observable (pkt/GRUT_CANDIDATE_M1.md:75-89,93-101; pkt/PREDICTIONS_AND_TESTS.md:81-83). Conditional constraint: all probabilities hold only at L=3-5 on sampled grids for pure prepared states at the benchmark; eps=0 theorem only at exact saturation; storage numbers only under post-scattering decoupling (pkt/WORKED_REGIME.md:95-97; pkt/PREDICTIONS_AND_TESTS.md:72-79; pkt/EXECUTION.md:88-94). Conjecture: an error-correction-by-cascade regime where facilitation helps against F=eps (rev/REVIEW.md:54; pkt/PREDICTIONS_AND_TESTS.md:85-88); relation to Candidate 1 as a low-energy sector (pkt/COMPATIBILITY.md:87-92). Unexecuted task: writer scattering on the uniform-floor core (pkt/control_protocol.json:16); cascade calculation (pkt/COMPATIBILITY.md:41-44); thermal-inclusion attacks (pkt/OPEN_CLAIMS.md:38-40); dynamical terminal detector and reset cost (pkt/OPEN_CLAIMS.md:48-49); interrupted dt=0.0001 local-Pauli replay, superseded not completed (pkt/EXECUTION.md:67-70); registered no-boundary B control referenced as still unexecuted (pkt/COMPATIBILITY.md:94-97).

**Remaining freedom.**
- Entire parameter point (eps, alpha, K, Delta, h_x, h_z); no calibration to seconds or to any physical system (pkt/WORKED_REGIME.md:6-7; pkt/PREDICTIONS_AND_TESTS.md:8-14,81-83)
- Topology (ring), dimensionality, size beyond L=5, record encoding, local versus optimal readout (pkt/GRUT_CANDIDATE_M1.md:80,139-142)
- Whether to keep the facilitation term at all: the packet retains the original M1 law 'rather than silently replaced by the better control' although the control retains better (pkt/GRUT_CANDIDATE_M1.md:151-152)
- A cascade successor needs 'noise, an error syndrome, correction dynamics, entropy/energy resources and an advantage over this matched baseline' (pkt/COMPATIBILITY.md:41-44)
- A gravitational successor must specify which horizon/scattering assumption it changes, compute a regulated gravitational observable and recover low-energy tests (pkt/COMPATIBILITY.md:79-83)
- Rejection rule for a calibrated instance: disagreement with full conditional traces beyond stated uncertainties rejects that calibrated model, not 'every idea called GRUT' (pkt/PREDICTIONS_AND_TESTS.md:45-49)
- Writer parameters and gate shapes are apparatus inputs; finite-clock errors for a uniform-floor writer remain uncalculated (pkt/PREDICTIONS_AND_TESTS.md:12-13; pkt/control_protocol.json:16)

**Failures retained.**
- Facilitation-benefit attribution withdrawn after the matched control (pkt/REPORT.md:3-8,137-139; pkt/OPEN_CLAIMS.md:16-19; AGENT_COORDINATION.md:5820)
- Original F=1 control confounded: twentyfold field increase, 'cannot isolate the benefit of neighbor dependence' (pkt/REPORT.md:32-33; rev/REVIEW.md:21)
- Single-cell retention fails 0.9 standard: 0.801481 (L=4), 0.751472 (L=5) at t=10 (pkt/WORKED_REGIME.md:155-157)
- Bare autonomous write fails: maxima 0.553352 and 0.526376 (pkt/WORKED_REGIME.md:158-160)
- Unconstrained-flip perturbations break the 0.9 condition: 0.875748 (kappa=0.1), 0.764940 (kappa=0.3) (pkt/WORKED_REGIME.md:161-165)
- At eps=0 the ordered sector cannot gain weight from a disordered superposition: a formation barrier (pkt/OPEN_CLAIMS.md:25-28)
- dt=0.0001 local-Pauli writer replay interrupted with Ctrl-C and replaced, not completed (pkt/EXECUTION.md:67-70)

### s11_toric_bridge — Toric memory / scrambling bridge (separate branch claude/toric4d-memory-vs-scrambling-20261019, commits 52b8f11 pre-registration and 8e0f89f results; not merged)  (`s11_toric_bridge`)

**Question asked.** Does bridge claim B ("a passive self-correcting memory is necessarily non-scrambling / non-thermalising in its local degrees of freedom, so M implies S") hold in the one translation-invariant model proven to be a passive self-correcting memory, the 4D toric code, when logical memory time and local thermalisation time are measured under the same thermal Metropolis dynamics? (tb:REPORT.md:7-13; tb:README.md:7-15). Under the parent closed-whole question this sector was a side test of one proposed internal link in the "memory" route, not a test of what a closed whole with internal baths forces.

**Assumptions / model class.**
- Checkout: task names 96a7e7e; working tree is 4a53fb2, a descendant; git diff --stat 96a7e7e HEAD on the handoff directory is empty, so the handoff text cited is identical to the handoff commit's.
- Model class: 4D toric code Z-error sector only, Z2 variables on faces of an L^4 torus, E = 2*|boundary of e|, logical readout on the X-bar membrane of plane (01) (tb:REPORT.md:17; tb:README.md:21; tb:code/toric4d.py:13-36).
- Dynamics: single-face Metropolis at fixed inverse temperature beta, 'Local, Markovian, detailed-balance; a Glauber/Davies-type bath' (tb:README.md:25; tb:code/toric4d.py:44-56) — an OPEN system with an external ideal heat bath, not a closed whole whose bath is its own complement.
- Decoder: zero-temperature local cooling for L^2 sweeps, 'a local decoder, not minimum-weight' (tb:README.md:27; tb:code/toric4d.py:82-95,115).
- Control: 2D toric code with MWPM (pymatching) readout, no ordered phase (tb:README.md:23).
- Quantum illustration: exact 18-qubit 2D toric code, h = 0.3, infinite-temperature OTOC by stochastic trace, pre-declared 'not decisive' and 'does not test B' (tb:README.md:29; tb:code/toric2d_quantum_otoc.py:1-5).
- Grid: 4D L in {3,4,5}, beta in {0.60,0.90,1.00,1.10}, 2D L in {4,6,8,12}, t_max = 1e5 sweeps, 18-point log time grid; trajectories as run 48/24/16 (4D) and 64 (2D) (tb:code/run_all.py:5; tb:REPORT.md:21).
- Pre-registered readings R1/R2/R3 with the rule 'No reading is changed after the numbers are seen' (tb:README.md:44-54); numeric thresholds operationalising them were written in analyze.py at results time (tb:code/analyze.py:111-125,137-146).
- Pre-registered exclusions: no quantum OTOC or Lyapunov exponent in the 4D code; spin-2 sector untouched; 'Nothing here concerns de Sitter space; the dS chaos results are taken from the literature brief' (tb:README.md:58-60).
- External input not in the repository: the 19 Oct 2026 literature brief supplying the de Sitter lambda_L values and the CLPW / 3D no-go / AdS/CFT arguments (tb:README.md:11,15; tb:REPORT.md:13,132,136).

- Literature brief supplying the de Sitter lambda_L values and other broader claims is absent from the repository on HEAD and on the toric branch — `git grep -i -E 'Aalsma|Kolchmeyer|Narovlansky|literature brief' on HEAD and origin/claude/toric4d-memory-vs-scrambling-20261019 (only unrelated hit: research/reality_framework/grut_closure_fork_20261019/SOURCES.md:50); tb:README.md:11` — Aalsma-Shiu 2020 lambda_L = 2pi/beta; Kolchmeyer-Liu 2024 and Narovlansky 2025 4pi/beta (as printed in README:11, unverified)
- Branch is unmerged; only 52b8f11 and 8e0f89f are branch-only; merge-base f28b49d; neither branch commit touches AGENT_COORDINATION.md — `git branch -a --contains 8e0f89f; git merge-base HEAD origin/claude/toric4d-memory-vs-scrambling-20261019; git log HEAD..branch; git show --stat 52b8f11 / 8e0f89f` — 7 commits in HEAD not on branch
- Handoff row and note: compares Metropolis trajectories and decoded memory, post-hoc gauge correction, separate quantum illustration, not a closed-unitary gravitational MSS computation; first 105 lines of the 144-line REPORT read; preserve distinction from 'the reviewer's broader claims' — `hand/NEXT_CHAT_HANDOFF.md:124,190-195; AGENT_COORDINATION.md:5828` — first 105 lines, REPORT.md = 144 lines

**Answer.** What was compared: in the classical single-face Metropolis dynamics of the 4D toric code Z-sector at fixed beta, the decoded logical half-life (O1, cooling decoder) against the equilibrium autocorrelation time of local observables (O2) and twin-copy damage spreading (O3), at the same (L, beta) but in separate seeded runs (tb:code/toric4d.py:198,202-203), with a 2D toric-code MWPM control (O5) and an unrelated exact 18-qubit infinite-temperature OTOC illustration (Q1). Result in that model: at beta >= 1.0 the logical half-life grows with L (977 -> 1.64e+04 -> 7.98e+04 sweeps at beta=1.0; 1.31e+04 -> >1e5 -> >1e5 at beta=1.1) while the edge-stabiliser autocorrelation crosses 1/e in 0.77-1.18 sweeps at every (L, beta) and an injected face flip heals to D=0 within one sweep; the 2D control stays at 1-4 sweeps for all L (tb:REPORT.md:39-60,78-93). The estimator change was from an exponential least-squares fit to a log-interpolated half-life, made after an unpreserved first production attempt, recorded in REPORT but not in README despite the commit message (tb:REPORT.md:21; empty README diff). The post-hoc correction removed the pre-registered face-spin observable from O2 as not an observable of the code (zero-energy stabiliser drift: flipped-face fraction 0.515625 at t=3e4 with energy per face 0.0), labelled post hoc; by the letter of the pre-registration R2 FAILS on that observable, and the packet's fallback rests on O1 + the secondary O3 (tb:REPORT.md:23,117-128). The separate quantum illustration shows an 18-qubit perturbed 2D stabiliser Hamiltonian scrambling at infinite temperature (OTOC 0.981 at t=8/J) with the logical autocorrelation also decaying (0.019); the packet states it does not bear on B (tb:REPORT.md:97-106). Confirmed: this is not a closed-unitary gravitational MSS-exponent computation — the dynamics is an open Davies-type Metropolis bath at fixed beta, no Lyapunov exponent, thermal OTOC, 2*pi*T comparison or gravitational degree of freedom appears in code or results, and the packet says so (tb:README.md:25,58; tb:REPORT.md:134). The report author's broader claims (de Sitter lambda_L, CLPW, 3D no-go, 'as holography predicted', 'retire the sharp reading') are recorded separately; they rest on a literature brief absent from the repository (tb:REPORT.md:132,136,140; tb:README.md:11).

**Closure verdict.** Nothing additional beyond known physics was forced in this sector, and the sector did not operate in the closed-whole class at all: the computation is of an open system (a lattice code in contact with an external ideal bath at fixed beta, tb:README.md:25), so it cannot speak to what a closed whole with internal baths forces. Within its own class it recovers known physics (thermal stability of the 4D toric code per DKLP 2002 / Alicki et al. 2010, L-independent 2D memory per Alicki-Fannes-Horodecki, fast decorrelation of local gauge-invariant observables), as the packet's own priority statement says: 'The only addition is the joint measurement of memory and local thermalisation as a test of B' (tb:REPORT.md:136). The computation that shows 'nothing additional' is the O1/O2/O3 table set (size-growing logical half-life 977 -> 1.64e+04 -> 7.98e+04 sweeps at beta=1.0 against ~1-sweep stabiliser decorrelation and one-sweep damage healing), i.e. a counterexample to bridge claim B in the classical 4D toric code. What this removes from GRUT is a proposed internal link (memory implies non-scrambling), not a law of physics; no new constraint, state-selection rule, chaos bound or gravitational statement is produced.

**Classification.** `mixed` — recovered known physics (4D toric-code thermal stability, L-independent 2D memory time, fast local thermalisation of stabilisers; acknowledged as prior literature at tb:REPORT.md:136 and tb:README.md:62) + rejected mechanism as a conditional constraint (bridge claim B rejected only in the classical Metropolis Z-sector 4D toric code, beta in {1.0, 1.1}, L in {3,4,5}, local cooling decoder, t_max 1e5, with the O2 verdict depending on a labelled post-hoc observable correction and the fallback resting on secondary O3; tb:REPORT.md:122-128) + supplied model choices (fixed-beta Metropolis bath, decoder, grid, seeds, half-life estimator, analyze.py numeric thresholds) + conjecture (the author's de Sitter / CLPW / holography / 'retire the sharp reading' claims, resting on an out-of-repository brief; tb:REPORT.md:132,136,140) + unexecuted tasks (quantum OTOC / Lyapunov exponent in the 4D code, spin-2 sector question, independent review; tb:REPORT.md:3,134).

**Remaining freedom.**
- Any quantum (unitary or Lindbladian) version of the 4D toric code: no OTOC or Lyapunov exponent computed; the R2-b escalation path to 'a quantum OTOC computation in a large-N self-correcting model' was never triggered or run (tb:README.md:50,58).
- Full code (X and Z sectors), non-Metropolis, non-Markovian, and any closed-system / internal-bath variant: unexamined; one sector and one Davies-type rule were used (tb:README.md:21,25).
- Temperatures outside beta in {0.6, 0.9, 1.0, 1.1}, sizes L > 5, times beyond 1e5 sweeps, more than 16 trajectories at L=5 (tb:REPORT.md:21,37).
- Decoder choice: the local cooling decoder is not minimum-weight; its failure mode is argued only to shorten memory time (tb:REPORT.md:134).
- Whether a passive self-correcting phase can carry a Lorentz-invariant spin-2 sector (claim M): untouched (tb:REPORT.md:134).
- Everything about de Sitter, the MSS bound and gravitational chaos: outside the packet (tb:README.md:60).

**Failures retained.**
- Pre-registered O2 face-spin observable was ill-posed ('not an observable of the code'), recognised only after the production runs; by the letter of the pre-registration R2 FAILS on it (tb:REPORT.md:23,117-120).

- Commit 8e0f89f message says the estimator change is 'recorded in README/REPORT', but README was not changed (empty git diff 52b8f11 8e0f89f -- README.md); README still describes the exponential fit and '16 trajectories per point' (tb:README.md:35,42).

- 2D damage-spreading results set aside as 'seed-dependent and not used' (tb:REPORT.md:93) though present in SUMMARY (tb:results/SUMMARY.md:60-75).
- 'Independent review outstanding' (tb:REPORT.md:3); branch unmerged; no coordination entry for it.
- REPORT/README phrase the design as 'in the same thermal trajectories' (tb:REPORT.md:13; tb:README.md:62) whereas the code measures O2 and O3 in separate seeded runs at the same (L, beta) (tb:code/toric4d.py:198,202-203).
- Numeric thresholds making R1/R2 'mechanical' were written in analyze.py at results time, not in the pre-registration (analyze.py new in 8e0f89f; tb:README.md:46-50).

- The energy-scan grid has no beta=0.85 point; 'between 0.85 and 0.95' is a reading between the 0.9 and 0.95 points (tb:results/energy_scan_prelim.json:61-68).

**Must not be promoted.**
- 'B is FALSE in the 4D toric code' must not become 'memory and non-scrambling are independent in general' or 'GRUT's memory route is refuted'; it holds in one classical stochastic sector of one model at two temperatures and three sizes (tb:REPORT.md:122,134).
- Must not be described as a Lyapunov-exponent computation, an MSS-bound test, a de Sitter result, or a closed-system / internal-bath result; it is none of these (tb:REPORT.md:134; tb:README.md:25,58,60).

- The de Sitter lambda_L values (2pi/beta, 4pi/beta), the CLPW collision and the 3D no-go theorems are imported from an unavailable brief and must not be entered as repository-verified facts (tb:README.md:11; tb:REPORT.md:132).

Register price: +16 net underived inputs, derived tier empty (POSTULATE_MAP.md:6,17-19,28,30,38-39; GRUT_PROGRAM_STATE_SYNTHESIS_01.md:62,84,190-200; GRUT_SUCCESSOR_STATUS_01.md:22,43; EA0_OWNER_RULING_02.md:51-53; GRUT_MINIMUM_CORE.md:19-22; GRUT_FINAL_SYNTHESIS_01.md:235-236). (iii) Access structure derived? No. "nothing selects the access seed; it is currently irreducible" (GRUT_PROGRAM_STATE_SYNTHESIS_01.md:72-73); the split is Bin 1 bedrock and F2 why-split is fenced below it (POSTULATE_MAP.md:17,89); EA-0 terminated UNFORMULABLE at earned Level-0 scope with the local net presupposed (EA0_OWNER_RULING_02.md:11,51-53); the successor bridge gate terminated NONUNIQUE-LIFT with lift selection IRREDUCIBLE/SUPPLIED (L0_ACCESS_BRIDGE_01.md:7; CURRENT_STATE.json:137-145); geometry reconstruction works only given sufficiently rich access, in class (GRUT_PROGRAM_STATE_SYNTHESIS_01.md:70-72). (iv) EA-0 concluded: UNFORMULABLE at the earned Level-0 scope because the earned substrate is classical deterministic and the reach/compression construction is undefined on it without an unearned lift; "not negatively answered"; CLASS-SPLIT rejected; subordinate non-upgrading quantum findings: faithful states TRIVIAL/IDENTITY, generic H non-selective at d=4,8 only, conditional FORMULABLE branch with Theorem S sufficient only and no iff; "canonical", the L7 mechanism and "exclusion, never inclusion" retired; EA-1 closed; S-5 migration "motivated, not forced" (EA0_OWNER_RULING_02.md:11-31,60-70,77-78; EA0_CORRECTIONS_01.md:39-50,69-107; EA0_INDEPENDENT_VERIFICATION_01.md:160-163).

**Closure verdict.** Nothing additional was forced in the stated classes, and this sector's own ledgers are the computation that shows it: (a) a 74-claim register (count reproduced this session) banking zero FORBIDDEN no-gos and pricing the stance at net +16 supplied inputs with an empty derived tier (NO_GO_LEDGER.md:13; POSTULATE_MAP.md:6-7; GRUT_FINAL_SYNTHESIS_01.md:235-236); (b) a prediction ledger that is EMPTY (GRUT_FINAL_SYNTHESIS_01.md:222-226); (c) the explicit finding that every kernel alternative was "excluded by an input, never by a principle" (GRUT_FINAL_SYNTHESIS_01.md:170-171); (d) EA-0's verified result that every seedless access candidate is global, generically trivial, a correlation readout, or generator-carried by identity (EA0_ENDOGENOUS_ACCESS_FORMULATION_01.md:140-144; EA0_INDEPENDENT_VERIFICATION_01.md:149), followed by UNFORMULABLE on the earned substrate and NONUNIQUE-LIFT on the bridge. The one object a closed-whole description must have — a specified subsystem/access structure — is in every record a supplied datum; closure, in the program's own ledgers, forces no pure state, bath temperature, kernel shape, arrow, state-selection rule, constant, or action; wherever one appears it is marked supplied, borrowed, primitive, conditional, or unresolved. This is not a proof that no future postulate could have content; the record refuses that promotion itself (EA0_ENDOGENOUS_ACCESS_FORMULATION_01.md:367-370; NO_GO_LEDGER.md:79). Pure global state and compact topology are simply absent from this sector's sources.

**Classification.** `mixed` — Dominant: supplied model choice (Bin 1 primitives, Bin 3 borrowings, access seed, lift, generator, local net, time parameter); rejected mechanism (alpha-bridge normalization, mu=4/3, single-parameter w(z) economy, L7 mechanism, 'canonical' Gamma, in-in->EH, decoherence->Born, any GRUT-internal kernel-selection principle, rung7<->rung9 unification); unexecuted task (universality-probe #1 DEFERRED, criterion-unification fork never run, EA-1 closed, S-5 held, three MUST-BE-REOPENED negatives, competitor screen not computable, Route A held); conditional constraint (gap->memory and passivity->positivity within C1-a; mu_linear under p_tt; L3' at d=4,8; Theorem S as sufficient; no-crossing rung3-gated); recovered known physics (KMS/FDT locking, worldline-EFT tidal structure, Mori-Zwanzig effective dissipation, dS graviton absorptive response with 'None is a GRUT discriminator'). No conjecture in this sector is promoted to a result.
